## `0043` — First Category II operational construct (`OperationalSession`) lands; `padded-v1` canonical operational definition; `internal/derivation` + `cmd/derive-operational-session` introduced

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** Charter §2.2 Epistemic Separation establishes Category II — operational constructs derived deterministically from Cat I observations under a versioned operational definition. [`§0015`](#0015--q1-resolution-declaredsession-cat-i-primary-operationalsession-cat-ii-operational-construct-adopted) Q1 resolution named `OperationalSession` as the canonical Category II construct. [`entity-model.md` §Category II](../ontology/entity-model.md) line 44 specified the structural-identity composition ("Identity composes the definition reference, its parameters, and its time scope") + line 45 the versioning rule ("Re-derivation under a new definition produces a new construct, never a mutation of the existing one") + line 46 the version-as-identity rule ("Definitions are versioned. The definition that produced a construct is part of its identity"). Until now the substrate held only Cat I primary observations + the `IngestionEvent` enrichment — no record was Cat II. The §2.2 epistemic-separation claim was therefore structural-but-empty: the ontology distinguished the categories but the implementation did not yet exhibit them at runtime. This entry lands the first Category II construct, making the §2.2 separation structurally falsifiable.

- **Decision:** Introduce **`OperationalSession`** as the first Category II operational construct, with three structural moves:

  1. **`OperationalSession` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/operational_session.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/operational_session.proto). Six fields: `definition_version` (string, identity component), `definition_parameters` (string in canonical form — lowercase `key=value` pairs sorted by key, separated by semicolons — also identity), `source_event_hash` (bytes, provenance reference to the source `DeclaredSession` per [Charter §2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity)), `actor_ref` (string, inherits from source per [entity-model.md line 36](../ontology/entity-model.md)), `operational_start_at` (int64 Unix ns), `operational_end_at` (int64 Unix ns). The boundary fields are the divergence surface per entity-model.md line 34 ("OperationalSession may diverge from its source DeclaredSession in boundary timing").

  2. **`internal/derivation` package** at [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/). Defines the `OperationalDefinition` interface (`Version() string`, `Parameters() string`, `Derive(source *DeclaredSession, sourceHash [32]byte) *OperationalSession`) — the deterministic-derivation contract. Per the §2.2 Category II requirement, non-deterministic implementations of `Derive` violate the contract; the test suite enforces this structurally by recomputing twice + comparing content-hashes. `DeriveAll(ctx, sub, def, now)` walks every `DeclaredSession` row in the substrate (skipping `IngestionEvent`, `NetworkEvent`, and prior `OperationalSession` rows), derives, and commits via `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu` per [`concurrency-pattern.md`](../architecture/concurrency-pattern.md) §Substrate-Writer Serialization). Re-derivation under an IDENTICAL `(version, parameters)` tuple is a no-op via content-hash collision; re-derivation under a NEW tuple produces NEW records (versioning) — the substrate preserves both.

  3. **`padded-v1` canonical operational definition** at [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go). Boundary derivation: `operational_start_at = source.declared_at`; `operational_end_at = source.declared_at + pad_seconds`. Identity-tier (`actor_ref`) inherits from source. Encodes parameters as `pad_seconds=<int>`. This is the minimal viable canonical example — it exercises every structural mechanism (deterministic derivation, versioning, provenance, boundary divergence, identity composition) without requiring multi-Cat-I-type ingestion. Sophisticated definitions (inactivity-window, multi-input via `NetworkEvent`, identity-tier override) extend the same `OperationalDefinition` pathway mechanically — the same five-step procedure that §0042 established for adding Cat I types.

  Operator interface: [`cmd/derive-operational-session`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/derive-operational-session/main.go). Third operational binary alongside `verify` + `orphan-cleanup`. Flags: `-db`, `-blobs`, `-definition-version` (default `padded-v1`), `-pad-seconds` (default 300). Structured JSON to stdout (`definition_version`, `definition_parameters`, `examined`, `newly_derived`, `already_derived`); brief human summary to stderr. Exit code: **0** on success (including zero-newly-derived); **2** on tool/configuration error.

  Operational shape:

  - **Derivation is operator-invoked**, not inline on `Append`. This decouples Cat II derivation cadence from ingestion throughput; matches the verify + orphan-cleanup pattern; lets operators choose when to materialize the current operational-definition's view of session boundaries.
  - **Cat II records live in the same substrate** (events table) as Cat I — substrate immutability ([Charter §2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity)) applies uniformly. Per `projection-model.md` projections are rebuildable views; OperationalSession is **not** a projection, it is a Cat II construct committed to the substrate per entity-model.md §Category II.
  - **Re-derivation under new parameters produces new records, not mutations.** The prior derivation is preserved; the operational record now carries multiple OperationalSessions for the same source DeclaredSession (one per `(definition_version, definition_parameters)` tuple). This is the versioning rule of entity-model.md line 45 enforced structurally.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [Charter §2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `OperationalSession` records are committed to the same substrate under the same content-addressed-immutability discipline; no Cat I record is mutated. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — `OperationalSession` is unambiguously Category II (operational construct; deterministic derivation; carries the operational-definition reference as identity); the type makes the §2.2 separation observable at runtime. Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — `source_event_hash` is the structural reference to the source Cat I observation; the reverse index is recoverable via SQL lookup. Respects [`§0015`](#0015--q1-resolution-declaredsession-cat-i-primary-operationalsession-cat-ii-operational-construct-adopted) Q1 — `OperationalSession` is the canonical Cat II construct. Respects [`§0023`](#0023--q2-resolution-inception-phase-single-tier-actor_ref-multi-tier-formalization-deferred-to-ordinary-ontology-rfc-discipline) Q2 — `actor_ref` remains single-tier; inheritance from source per entity-model.md line 36 is the default. Respects [`§0027`](#0027--inception-phase-storage-technology-selection-sqlite--content-addressed-blob-store-on-local-filesystem-adopted-third-and-final-technology-rfc-per-0022-pivot--0003-fully-discharged) AP6 — content-addressed idempotency under `INSERT OR IGNORE` makes re-derivation safe. Respects [`§0029`](#0029--concurrency-pattern-architecture-document-introduced-0025-modification-5-discharged) — `DeriveAll` reads via `WalkEvents` (no `writeMu`) + writes via `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu`). Respects the canonical-vocabulary discipline at [`CLAUDE.md` §3](../../.claude/CLAUDE.md) — operational session, operational construct, operational definition, deterministic, derivation, versioned, provenance, substrate are all used as written.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/operational_session.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/operational_session.proto) — new Cat II message type. Six fields; constitutional anchors in the file header.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended to include `events/v1/operational_session.proto`; new `derive-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go) — new package. `OperationalDefinition` interface; `Report`; `DeriveAll` walker.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go) — first canonical operational definition.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation_test.go) — 8 tests (Derive determinism, boundary divergence, actor_ref inheritance, happy-path DeriveAll over 3 sources, re-derivation idempotency, versioning under changed parameters, empty substrate, walker skips non-DeclaredSession rows).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/derive-operational-session/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/derive-operational-session/main.go) — new binary; third operational CLI alongside `verify` + `orphan-cleanup`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIRecords` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing Cat II records; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across categories).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"operational-session"` entry.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/operational-session-minimal.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) + `operational-session-padded-v1.{json,bin,hash}` — **6 new corpus files** (2 entries × {json,bin,hash}). Mirrors the Cat I coverage shape: one all-defaults entry, one production-shaped entry exercising every field. The padded-v1 entry uses a synthetic 32-byte `source_event_hash` so the corpus is self-contained.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status section + entries table updated for the Cat II type.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`derive-operational-session` CLI section; §Category I message types updated with the Cat II addition note.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0043 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **125 tests** across the service (up from 114 at [`§0042`](#0042--second-category-i-message-type-networkevent-added-polymorphic-dispatch-registry-0030-follow-on-partially-discharged); +11 = 8 derivation + 1 verify-with-Cat-II + 2 corpus subtests). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Third operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains: `verify` ([`§0039`](#0039--verify-cli-tool-added-0033-verify-follow-on-discharged)), `orphan-cleanup` ([`§0041`](#0041--orphan-blob-cleanup-cli-added-with-operator-confirmation-discipline-0040-follow-on-discharged)), `derive-operational-session` (this entry). The `cmd/<name>/main.go` pattern continues to scale for per-service operational tooling.
  - **§2.2 Epistemic Separation is now structurally observable at runtime.** Before this entry, every substrate row was Cat I (DeclaredSession, NetworkEvent, IngestionEvent). After this entry, the substrate carries Cat II records (`message_type` = `ghosttrace.events.v1.OperationalSession`) that reference Cat I sources by content-hash. A consumer can distinguish Cat I from Cat II by reading `message_type`; the constitutional separation is no longer a property only of the ontology document.
  - **Smoke test of the binary.** `bin/derive-operational-session -db /tmp/empty.db -blobs /tmp/empty-blobs` on an empty substrate emits `{"definition_version":"padded-v1","definition_parameters":"pad_seconds=300","examined":0,"newly_derived":0,"already_derived":0}` and exits 0 — confirmed at commit time.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Multi-Cat-I-input operational definitions** — `padded-v1` derives from a single `DeclaredSession`. An `inactivity-window-v1` definition that consumes `NetworkEvent` rows for the same `actor_ref` within a time window is the natural follow-on; it would exercise the §0042 dispatch + the §0043 derivation pathway together. Deferred.
    - **Identity-tier override** — per entity-model.md line 36, the operational definition MAY override `actor_ref` when the operational reading differs from the declared. `padded-v1` inherits; an override-capable definition is deferred.
    - **Per-DeclaredSession derivation diff** — surfacing the structural divergence between source `declared_at` and derived `operational_*_at` as a first-class structural indicator (per [`entity-model.md` line 34](../ontology/entity-model.md), the divergences are themselves first-class structural). Deferred until operational consumers exist.
    - **Re-derivation triggered by ingestion-side events** — `derive-operational-session` is operator-invoked, not inline. A subscription-style trigger (derive on every new DeclaredSession) would couple Cat II derivation to ingestion throughput; deferred.
    - **OperationalSession assertions** — per [Charter §1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#1-thesis) + provenance-model.md, assertions reference Cat II constructs via `subject_ref_construct` edges. No assertion record type exists yet; deferred to the first concrete assertion-bearing pathway.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry is the first concrete realization of the Cat II discipline named in [`§0015`](#0015--q1-resolution-declaredsession-cat-i-primary-operationalsession-cat-ii-operational-construct-adopted); no prior decision reversed. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied; this entry proceeds under ordinary RFC discipline.

## `0044` — Second operational definition (`inactivity-window-v1`); `DerivationContext` interface; §0042 + §0043 composition proven

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced) landed the first Cat II construct with the minimal `padded-v1` operational definition — sufficient to prove every Cat II structural mechanism (determinism, versioning, provenance, boundary divergence) but contrived: the boundary derived from a single `DeclaredSession` alone. [`§0042`](#0042--second-category-i-message-type-networkevent-added-polymorphic-dispatch-registry-0030-follow-on-partially-discharged) added `NetworkEvent` as the second Cat I type but no operational definition consumed it. The two threads had not been composed: §0043's claim that "sophisticated definitions extend the same `OperationalDefinition` pathway mechanically" was unfalsified. This entry composes them by landing `inactivity-window-v1`, the canonical multi-Cat-I-input operational definition from [`entity-model.md` line 39](../ontology/entity-model.md) ("events from one actor within a 30-minute inactivity window").

- **Decision:** Land `inactivity-window-v1` + extend the `OperationalDefinition` contract with `DerivationContext` for typed access to additional Cat I observations. Three structural moves:

  1. **`DerivationContext` interface** at [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go). Provides typed accessors to additional Cat I observations a definition may consult while deriving a single `OperationalSession`. First accessor: `NetworkEventsForActor(actorRef string) []*eventsv1.NetworkEvent` returning the per-actor list sorted ascending by `observed_at`. New typed accessors are added here as new operational definitions require new Cat I observation types — the same five-step extensibility procedure that §0042 established for adding Cat I types and §0043 established for adding operational definitions.

  2. **`OperationalDefinition.Derive` signature extended** to take `(source, sourceHash, dctx DerivationContext)`. Backward-equivalent change for `padded-v1` (the definition ignores `dctx`); admits multi-input definitions like `inactivity-window-v1`. The `DeriveAll` walker now runs in two passes: pass 1 pre-collects every `NetworkEvent` grouped by `actor_ref` (each group sorted by `observed_at`) into a `walkerContext` that implements `DerivationContext`; pass 2 walks `DeclaredSession` rows and derives via the supplied context. Pre-collection is O(N) once per `DeriveAll` invocation; per-derivation lookup is O(1) map access. Determinism is preserved: the same substrate state + the same definition parameters produces an identical `OperationalSession`.

  3. **`inactivity-window-v1` operational definition** at [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1.go). Boundary algorithm (deterministic):

     - `lastObserved := source.declared_at`.
     - For each `NetworkEvent` with matching `actor_ref` + `observed_at ≥ declared_at`, in `observed_at`-ascending order:
       - if `observed_at - lastObserved ≤ InactivitySeconds * 1e9`: `lastObserved = observed_at`.
       - else: stop (the gap exceeded the window; the operational session ended).
     - `operational_end_at = lastObserved + InactivitySeconds * 1e9`.

     Parameters: `inactivity_seconds=<int>` (default 1800 = 30 min, matching the entity-model.md canonical example). Identity-tier (`actor_ref`) inherits from source per entity-model.md line 36. When no NetworkEvents match the actor (anonymous-collector case or no events yet), the derivation falls back to `lastObserved = declared_at`, equivalent in shape to a padded boundary but identity-distinct from any `padded-v1` derivation (different `definition_version`).

  Determinism caveat made explicit: `inactivity-window-v1` outputs depend on the substrate's `NetworkEvent` set at derivation time. New NetworkEvents arriving AFTER a derivation produce a DIFFERENT (newly-content-hashed) `OperationalSession` on re-derivation; the prior record is preserved per [entity-model.md line 45](../ontology/entity-model.md). This is the §0043 versioning principle applied to input-set changes, not just parameter changes: the substrate accumulates the operational record across both axes.

  Operator interface: `cmd/derive-operational-session` extended with a `-inactivity-seconds` command-line option + registry entry. Invocation: `./bin/derive-operational-session -definition-version inactivity-window-v1 -inactivity-seconds 1800`.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `inactivity-window-v1` does not mutate any Cat I record; it reads NetworkEvents + DeclaredSessions + writes OperationalSessions, all under content-addressed-immutability. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — deterministic derivation per Cat II requirement; the boundary algorithm is total over its inputs (no randomness, no time-of-day dependence, no global state). Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — the OperationalSession's `source_event_hash` continues to reference the source `DeclaredSession`; the contributing NetworkEvents are recoverable by index lookup on `actor_ref` + `observed_at` range over the substrate. Respects [`§0029`](#0029--concurrency-pattern-architecture-document-introduced-0025-modification-5-discharged) — the two-pass walker reads via `WalkEvents` (no `writeMu`) + writes via `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu`). Respects [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced) — the `OperationalDefinition` contract evolution is contained (one package, one consumer); the §0043 `padded-v1` behavior is preserved under the extended signature. No new canonical vocabulary introduced (inactivity, window, gap are operational vocabulary; not Charter-level concepts).

- **Consequences:**
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation.go) — `DerivationContext` interface added; `OperationalDefinition.Derive` signature extended; `DeriveAll` runs in two passes (NetworkEvent pre-collection + derivation pass) via the new `walkerContext` type.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/padded_v1.go) — `Derive` signature updated; behavior unchanged (ignores `dctx`).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1.go) — new file. Second canonical operational definition.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/inactivity_window_v1_test.go) — 8 tests covering determinism, boundary extension, gap behavior, no-events fallback, cross-actor isolation, pre-session event exclusion, versioning under changed parameters, and full DeriveAll integration over a substrate with mixed DeclaredSession + NetworkEvent rows.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/derivation/derivation_test.go) — existing test `PaddedV1.Derive` call sites updated for the extended signature (pass `nil` `DerivationContext`); semantics unchanged.
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/derive-operational-session/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/derive-operational-session/main.go) — `-inactivity-seconds` command-line option added; `resolveDefinition` extended with the `inactivity-window-v1` registry entry.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — derive CLI usage example extended; registered-definitions table extended with the new row.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0044 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **133 tests** across the service (up from 125 at [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced); +8 inactivity-window-v1 tests). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **§0042 + §0043 composition is now structurally proven.** The integration test (`TestDeriveAllInactivityWindowV1Integration`) populates a substrate with a `DeclaredSession` + two `NetworkEvent` rows, runs `DeriveAll` with `inactivity-window-v1`, and asserts the resulting `OperationalSession`'s boundary reflects only the first network event (the second exceeds the inactivity window). Both Cat I types from §0042 and the Cat II derivation pathway from §0043 are exercised by a single end-to-end path.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** `bin/derive-operational-session -definition-version inactivity-window-v1 -inactivity-seconds 1800 -db /tmp/empty.db -blobs /tmp/empty-blobs` emits `{"definition_version":"inactivity-window-v1","definition_parameters":"inactivity_seconds=1800","examined":0,"newly_derived":0,"already_derived":0}` and exits 0 — confirmed at commit time.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Identity-tier override** — per [`entity-model.md` line 36](../ontology/entity-model.md), the operational definition MAY override `actor_ref` when the operational reading differs from the declared. Neither `padded-v1` nor `inactivity-window-v1` overrides; an override-capable definition is deferred.
    - **Cross-type input expansion** — `DerivationContext` currently exposes only `NetworkEventsForActor`. Future definitions consuming fingerprint snapshots or external authoritative state changes will extend the interface with new typed accessors. Deferred until those Cat I types exist.
    - **DerivationContext caching across DeriveAll invocations** — pass-1 re-collects NetworkEvents on every invocation. Caching across runs is operationally attractive for large substrates but requires substrate-change-detection machinery. Deferred until empirical pressure justifies.
    - **Streaming derivation** — current `DeriveAll` buffers the full NetworkEvent index in memory. For very large substrates a streaming approach (window of recent NetworkEvents) would reduce memory pressure. Deferred.
    - **Cat III hypothesis types** — the next constitutional move. Charter §2.5 (frozen) defines six lifecycle operations; implementing a Cat III subtype is multi-landing work. The Cat II pathway is now consolidated, reducing risk for the Cat III move.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced) `OperationalDefinition` contract (additive change to `Derive`'s signature; backward-equivalent for `padded-v1`); no prior decision reversed.

---

## `0045` — First Category III hypothesis subtype (`BehavioralCluster`) formation lands; §2.5 Hypothesis Lifecycle Explicitness structurally observable

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** Charter §2.5 Hypothesis Lifecycle Explicitness is FROZEN at v0.3 per [`§0013`](#0013--charter-25-frozen-v03-via-gate-25-pilot-redaction--four-lifecycle-operations-defined-glossary-extended). [`§0010`](#0010--q2-resolution-category-iii-hypothesis-decomposes-into-four-concrete-subtypes-behavioralcluster-coordinationring-campaignhypothesis-automationgroup) Q2 resolution names four concrete Cat III subtypes; [`entity-model.md` line 65](../ontology/entity-model.md) defines `BehavioralCluster` as "a set of actors whose behavioral patterns suggest operation by a common underlying entity." Cat III has constitutional ground (frozen invariant + ontological surface) but no implementation — every substrate row before this entry was Category I (DeclaredSession, NetworkEvent, IngestionEvent) or Category II (OperationalSession). The §2.5 separation-via-lifecycle claim was structural-but-empty: the ontology distinguished the lifecycle operations but the implementation did not yet exhibit any of them at runtime. This entry lands the first Cat III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterFormation`), making §2.5 structurally observable.

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralCluster` formation under Charter §2.5 + entity-model.md §Category III. Three structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterFormation` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_formation.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_formation.proto). Six fields: `pattern_signature` (string, identity component, e.g. `"session-descriptor-shared-v1"`), `pattern_parameters` (canonical-form string, identity), `actor_refs` (repeated string sorted ASCENDING for content-hash stability — entity-model.md line 65 "actor membership representation"), `formation_at` (int64 Unix ns — derived deterministically from inputs, NOT wall-clock at recording time), `confidence` (float placeholder pending [Charter §2.6](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#26-evidential-independence-integrity)), `source_event_hashes` (repeated bytes sorted ASCENDING — observational provenance per [Charter §2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity)). Per Charter §2.5 BC5, this record is a Category I substrate row under §2.1 immutability — the hypothesis's identity IS the formation event's content-hash. Per Charter §2.5 BC3, NO standalone `BehavioralCluster` "current-state" record is committed to the substrate; the hypothesis state is reconstructed by replaying the lifecycle event chain (projection layer deferred).

  2. **`internal/hypothesis` package** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/). Defines `FormationPattern` interface (`Signature() string`, `Parameters() string`, `Form(fctx FormationContext, formationAt int64) []*BehavioralClusterFormation`) — the deterministic-recording contract for Cat III formation. Parallel to [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced) `OperationalDefinition` for Cat II. `FormationContext` provides typed access to substrate observations (`DeclaredSessions() []SourceDeclaredSession`), parallel to the [`§0044`](#0044--second-operational-definition-inactivity-window-v1-derivationcontext-interface-0042--0043-composition-proven) `DerivationContext`. `FormAll(ctx, sub, pattern, now)` walks the substrate, runs `pattern.Form`, and commits each resulting formation event via `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu` per [`concurrency-pattern.md`](../architecture/concurrency-pattern.md) §Substrate-Writer Serialization). Re-running with identical `(pattern_signature, pattern_parameters, substrate-state)` is a no-op via content-hash collision; a NEW tuple element produces NEW lifecycle events (versioning per [entity-model.md line 45](../ontology/entity-model.md)).

  3. **`session-descriptor-shared-v1` formation pattern** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/session_descriptor_shared_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/session_descriptor_shared_v1.go). Algorithm:

     - Group every `DeclaredSession` in the substrate by byte-equal `session_descriptor` payload.
     - For each group, collect distinct `actor_refs` (empty `actor_ref` excluded — an actor must be identifiable to participate).
     - If the distinct-actor count >= `MinClusterSize`, emit one `BehavioralClusterFormation` with the sorted `actor_refs`, the sorted contributing `source_event_hashes`, `formation_at = max(declared_at across cluster)` (deterministic from inputs), and `confidence = 1.0 - 1.0/cluster_size` (placeholder pending §2.6).

     Parameters: `min_cluster_size=<int>` (default 2). The "shared session_descriptor → shared underlying operator" inference is the canonical example: minimum viable to prove every Cat III structural mechanism, parallel to [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced)'s `padded-v1` for Cat II. Sophisticated patterns (multi-Cat-I-input via `NetworkEvent`, fuzzy similarity, temporal windowing, identity-tier override) extend the same `FormationPattern` pathway mechanically.

  Operator interface: [`cmd/form-hypothesis`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/form-hypothesis/main.go). Fourth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`. Command-line options: `-pattern-signature` (default `session-descriptor-shared-v1`), `-min-cluster-size` (default 2). Structured JSON output (`pattern_signature`, `pattern_parameters`, `examined`, `newly_formed`, `already_formed`); human stderr summary. Exit codes: 0 / 2 (success / tool-config error).

  Determinism + non-determinism explicit:

  - The **recording** of a formation event is deterministic — given `(substrate-state, pattern_signature, pattern_parameters)`, the resulting `BehavioralClusterFormation` content-hash is reproducible. `formation_at` is derived from inputs (`max declared_at across cluster`), NOT from wall-clock at recording time, so re-running on the same inputs produces the same hypothesis identity.
  - The **inference** that triggers formation is non-deterministic per Charter §2.2 Cat III requirement: different `FormationPattern` implementations operating on the same substrate produce different hypotheses. The non-determinism lives in the **choice of pattern**, not in the recording mechanism.

  Acknowledgments of pending sections:

  - **§2.4 Inferential Influence Disclosure (pending — empirical pressure phase)** — `BehavioralClusterFormation` does not carry an `influenced_by_refs` field. When §2.4 redacts, this Protobuf message will extend additively to record cross-hypothesis influence declarations. Formation events written before §2.4 redaction remain interpretable: the absence of an influence field is empty-influence, consistent with the formation being inferred directly from observations.
  - **§2.6 Evidential Independence Integrity (pending — empirical pressure phase)** — `confidence` is a scalar placeholder. When §2.6 redacts, this Protobuf message will extend additively to record the paired `evidential_independence` dimension per [Charter §1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#1-thesis) + [`entity-model.md` line 58](../ontology/entity-model.md). The placeholder formula (`1.0 - 1.0/cluster_size`) is documented as such; §2.6 will refine semantics.

  Deferred lifecycle operations per Charter §2.5 (promotion, demotion, merge, split, dissolution): each lands as a separate Category I lifecycle-event record type referencing the originating `BehavioralClusterFormation`'s content-hash. Per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred), promotion MUST carry the Layer A cadence parameter (Layer B remains deferred until §2.6 operationalization). The same operator-invoked CLI pattern extends to drive each operation under follow-on landings; this entry establishes the formation pathway as the structural foundation.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterFormation` is committed under content-addressed immutability; no Cat I record is mutated. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — the formation is unambiguously Cat III (probabilistic construction); the recording is type-distinct from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs. Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — `source_event_hashes` carries the observational-provenance chain back to Cat I primaries. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) BC3 — NO `BehavioralCluster` "current-state" record is committed; only the lifecycle event. Respects §2.5 BC5 — the formation event is a Cat I record under §2.1. Respects [`§0010`](#0010--q2-resolution-category-iii-hypothesis-decomposes-into-four-concrete-subtypes-behavioralcluster-coordinationring-campaignhypothesis-automationgroup) Q2 — `BehavioralCluster` is one of the four named Cat III subtypes. Respects [`§0023`](#0023--q2-resolution-inception-phase-single-tier-actor_ref-multi-tier-formalization-deferred-to-ordinary-ontology-rfc-discipline) — `actor_ref` is single-tier. Respects [`§0029`](#0029--concurrency-pattern-architecture-document-introduced-0025-modification-5-discharged) — `FormAll` walks via `WalkEvents` (no `writeMu`) + writes via `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu`). Canonical vocabulary used as written: behavioral cluster, formation, hypothesis, lifecycle event, substrate, provenance, observation.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_formation.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_formation.proto) — new Cat III lifecycle event. Six fields; constitutional anchors in the file header.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `form-hypothesis-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/formation.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/formation.go) — new package. `FormationPattern` interface; `FormationContext` interface; `SourceDeclaredSession` pair type; `Report`; `FormAll` walker.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/session_descriptor_shared_v1.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/session_descriptor_shared_v1.go) — first canonical formation pattern.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/formation_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/formation_test.go) — 8 tests covering happy-path formation, min-cluster-size respect, idempotency, versioning under changed parameters, empty-actor exclusion, empty substrate, multi-group formation, placeholder confidence.
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/form-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/form-hypothesis/main.go) — new binary; fourth operational CLI.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIRecords` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing Cat III lifecycle events; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across all three Categories per §2.5 BC5).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-formation"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-formation-{minimal,shared-descriptor}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files** (2 entries × {json,bin,hash}). Mirrors the established coverage shape: one all-defaults entry, one production-shaped entry exercising every field. `source_event_hashes` uses synthetic 32-byte patterns so the corpus is self-contained.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status section + entries table updated for the Cat III lifecycle event.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`form-hypothesis` CLI section; §Cat I message types updated with the Cat III lifecycle-event-as-Cat-I note.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0045 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **142 tests** across the service (up from 133 at [`§0044`](#0044--second-operational-definition-inactivity-window-v1-derivationcontext-interface-0042--0043-composition-proven); +9 = 8 hypothesis + 1 verify-with-Cat-III + 2 corpus subtests). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Fourth operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains: `verify` ([`§0039`](#0039--verify-cli-tool-added-0033-verify-follow-on-discharged)), `orphan-cleanup` ([`§0041`](#0041--orphan-blob-cleanup-cli-added-with-operator-confirmation-discipline-0040-follow-on-discharged)), `derive-operational-session` ([`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-operationalsession-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced)), `form-hypothesis` (this entry).
  - **§2.5 Hypothesis Lifecycle Explicitness is now structurally observable at runtime.** Before this entry, every substrate row was Cat I or Cat II. After this entry, the substrate carries Cat III lifecycle events (`message_type` = `ghosttrace.events.v1.BehavioralClusterFormation`) committed under §2.1 per §2.5 BC5. A consumer reading the substrate can distinguish Cat III lifecycle events from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs by `message_type`; the §2.5 explicitness claim is no longer a property only of the ontology document.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** `bin/form-hypothesis -db /tmp/empty.db -blobs /tmp/empty-blobs` on an empty substrate emits `{"pattern_signature":"session-descriptor-shared-v1","pattern_parameters":"min_cluster_size=2","examined":0,"newly_formed":0,"already_formed":0}` and exits 0 — confirmed at commit time.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Promotion lifecycle event** (`BehavioralClusterPromotion`) — per Charter §2.5 + [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred), MUST record the Layer A cadence parameter governing subsequent demotion-candidacy. Layer B deep-criterion remains deferred until §2.6 operationalization.
    - **Demotion / merge / split / dissolution lifecycle events** — each lands as a separate Cat I record type per §2.5 (merge MUST reference all antecedent hypotheses; split MUST reference the antecedent + each successor).
    - **Other Cat III subtypes** — `CoordinationRing`, `CampaignHypothesis`, `AutomationGroup` per [`§0010`](#0010--q2-resolution-category-iii-hypothesis-decomposes-into-four-concrete-subtypes-behavioralcluster-coordinationring-campaignhypothesis-automationgroup) Q2. Each adds a new set of lifecycle-event record types (Formation, Promotion, Demotion, Merge, Split, Dissolution per subtype). Cross-subtype merge per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) produces a typed output record; resolution deferred to `lifecycle-semantics.md` post-Q4 redaction.
    - **Multi-Cat-I-input formation patterns** — `session-descriptor-shared-v1` derives only from `DeclaredSession`. A `network-cohort-v1` pattern consuming `NetworkEvent` rows (e.g. shared endpoint, temporal co-occurrence) would exercise the [`§0042`](#0042--second-category-i-message-type-networkevent-added-polymorphic-dispatch-registry-0030-follow-on-partially-discharged) dispatch + the formation pathway together — parallel to the §0044 inactivity-window-v1 composition for Cat II. Deferred.
    - **Projection over the lifecycle event chain** — per Charter §2.5 BC3, the hypothesis's current state is a projection over its lifecycle events. The projection layer (read-side materialization) is deferred; consumers needing current state today must replay the event chain themselves.
    - **§2.4 + §2.6 redaction triggers** — `BehavioralClusterFormation` is the first Cat III artifact whose concrete shape can pressure §2.4 (influence) and §2.6 (evidential independence) into redaction. The placeholder `confidence` field is the empirical pressure point that §2.6 must address.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry is the first concrete realization of the Cat III discipline named in Charter §2.5 + [`§0010`](#0010--q2-resolution-category-iii-hypothesis-decomposes-into-four-concrete-subtypes-behavioralcluster-coordinationring-campaignhypothesis-automationgroup) + [`§0013`](#0013--charter-25-frozen-v03-via-gate-25-pilot-redaction--four-lifecycle-operations-defined-glossary-extended); no prior decision reversed. The implementation-gate criteria established at [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) continue to be satisfied; this entry proceeds under ordinary RFC discipline.

## `0046` — Second Category III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterPromotion`) lands; Layer A cadence per §0011 materialized; §2.5 lifecycle now plural-observable

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) landed the FIRST Cat III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterFormation`) and made Charter §2.5 Hypothesis Lifecycle Explicitness structurally observable at a single point: formation. Charter §2.5 (frozen v0.3) names SIX lifecycle operations (formation, merge, split, promotion, demotion, dissolution); a single-point implementation leaves the "lifecycle explicitness" claim unfalsified across the five remaining operations. [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) (Q4 resolution) bound promotion specifically: "Promotion events carry the structural parameters governing the promoted hypothesis's subsequent demotion-candidacy. Layer A cadence parameter is MANDATORY; Layer B deep criterion remains deferred until §2.6 operationalization." Without a promotion lifecycle event in the substrate, §0011's structural commitment is also unfalsified. This entry lands `BehavioralClusterPromotion`, brings §2.5 to two observable operations, and materializes §0011's Layer A parameter requirement.

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralClusterPromotion` as the second Cat III lifecycle event, with three structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterPromotion` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_promotion.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_promotion.proto). Four fields:

     - `formation_event_hash` (bytes) — content-hash of the target `BehavioralClusterFormation`, identifying the hypothesis being promoted per the [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) identity-via-content-hash convention.
     - `promoted_at` (int64 Unix ns) — the timestamp from which Layer A cadence is measured.
     - `cadence_seconds` (int64) — Layer A parameter per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred); elapsed time since `promoted_at` that opens demotion-candidacy. MANDATORY per Charter §2.5 Structural Requirement.
     - `reason` (string) — optional operator-supplied forensic note.

     Per Charter §2.5 BC5 the promotion event is a Category I substrate row under §2.1 immutability.

  2. **`hypothesis.Promote(ctx, sub, opts, now)` entry point** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go). Operation shape distinct from `FormAll` (which walks the substrate deriving multiple formations): promotion is point-pick — operator specifies a single `formation_event_hash` and the function records exactly one promotion event. Validation:

     - `cadence_seconds > 0` (rejects zero/negative).
     - `substrate.LookupRow(formation_event_hash)` MUST succeed (otherwise `ErrTargetNotFound`).
     - The row's `message_type` MUST be `ghosttrace.events.v1.BehavioralClusterFormation` (otherwise `ErrTargetWrongType`) — preserves §2.5-lifecycle-integrity: promotion references only formations, never observations or constructs.

     Commit path: `substrate.Append` (acquires `writeMu` per concurrency-pattern.md §Substrate-Writer Serialization). Idempotent under identical `(formation_event_hash, promoted_at, cadence_seconds, reason)` via content-hash collision per §0027 AP6.

  3. **`cmd/promote-hypothesis` CLI** at [`services/ingestion/cmd/promote-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/promote-hypothesis/main.go). Fifth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`. Command-line options: `-formation-event-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-cadence-seconds` (default 86400 = 24 h), `-promoted-at-ns` (default 0 = wall-clock now), `-reason` (optional). Structured JSON output + human stderr summary. Exit codes: **0** success, **2** tool/configuration error, **3** target-not-found or target-wrong-type (the two §2.5-integrity errors).

  Wall-clock vs. determinism tradeoff made explicit:

  - With default `promoted_at=0`, each invocation records a wall-clock-time promotion (intentionally non-idempotent at fine granularity — each operator decision is its own event).
  - With explicit `-promoted-at-ns`, recording is fully deterministic — used for forensic replay + tests where the same operator decision needs to reproduce.
  - Re-running with identical args (including explicit `promoted_at`) is idempotent via content-hash collision; re-running with a DIFFERENT `cadence_seconds` produces a NEW promotion event alongside the prior one per §2.5 immutability (the operation history records every parameter change).

  Acknowledgments of pending sections:

  - **§2.4 Inferential Influence Disclosure (pending — empirical pressure phase)** — `BehavioralClusterPromotion` does not carry an `influenced_by_refs` field. Promotion is a structural state transition, not a new inferential assertion in the §1 sense; if §2.4 redaction concludes that promotion events ALSO require influence declarations (e.g., declaring which prior hypotheses' promotions influenced this operator decision), the Protobuf message will extend additively.
  - **§2.6 Evidential Independence Integrity (pending — empirical pressure phase)** — Layer B of the [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) staged-combination criterion is the structural test on evidential independence that §2.6 will operationalize. Until §2.6 redacts, the promotion event carries Layer A only; Layer B will arrive as an additive Protobuf field (or as parameters on the future `BehavioralClusterDemotion` event) when §2.6 lands.

  Deferred lifecycle operations remaining after this entry: demotion, merge, split, dissolution. Per Charter §2.5 each lands as a separate Cat I lifecycle-event record type; merge MUST reference all antecedent hypotheses, split MUST reference the antecedent + each successor.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterPromotion` committed under content-addressed immutability; no prior record mutated. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — promotion is a Cat III lifecycle operation; type-distinct from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) Structural Requirement — promotion event carries Layer A cadence parameter per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred). Respects §2.5 BC3 — no "current-state" hypothesis record committed; only the lifecycle event. Respects §2.5 BC5 — the promotion event IS a Cat I record under §2.1. Respects [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) — `cadence_seconds` (Layer A) is mandatory per the proto's structural shape and enforced at `Promote` entry. Respects [`§0029`](#0029--concurrency-pattern-architecture-document-introduced-0025-modification-5-discharged) — `Promote` acquires `writeMu` via `substrate.Append`. Respects [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) — promotion references the formation event's content-hash as the hypothesis identifier (no separate UUID generation; identity remains content-derived). Canonical vocabulary used as written: promotion, demotion, lifecycle event, hypothesis, substrate, provenance.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_promotion.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_promotion.proto) — new Cat III lifecycle event. Four fields; constitutional anchors in the file header.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `promote-hypothesis-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go) — new file. `PromoteOptions`, `PromoteReport`, `ErrTargetNotFound`, `ErrTargetWrongType`, `Promote` entry point.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion_test.go) — 7 tests covering happy-path, idempotency, versioning under changed cadence, zero-cadence rejection, unknown-target error, wrong-type-target error, default-promoted-at fallback.
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/promote-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/promote-hypothesis/main.go) — new binary; fifth operational CLI.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIPromotion` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing BOTH formation AND promotion lifecycle events; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across all Cat III lifecycle operations).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-promotion"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-promotion-{minimal,typical}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files**. Mirrors the established Cat III coverage shape.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status + entries table updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`promote-hypothesis` CLI section; §form-hypothesis updated to note promotion is the next operation in the chain.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0046 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **154 tests** across the service (up from 142 at [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable); +12 = 7 promotion + 1 verify-with-promotion + 2 corpus subtests + helpers). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Fifth operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains five binaries.
  - **§2.5 Hypothesis Lifecycle Explicitness is now PLURAL-observable at runtime.** Before this entry, the substrate carried only formation events for the Cat III chain. After this entry, formation + promotion events coexist; a consumer walking the substrate can reconstruct the partial lifecycle state of any BehavioralCluster (formation present + promotion present + cadence_seconds → demotion-candidacy timing per §0011).
  - **§0011 Layer A is materialized in the substrate.** Every promotion event carries `cadence_seconds`; the staged-combination criterion's first clause is now structurally checkable by any consumer.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/promote-hypothesis` without `-formation-event-hash` exits 2 with the required-argument error; (b) `bin/promote-hypothesis -formation-event-hash 00...00 -db /tmp/empty.db -blobs /tmp/empty-blobs` exits 3 with `ErrTargetNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Demotion lifecycle event** (`BehavioralClusterDemotion`) — natural follow-on. The §0011 staged-combination criterion's Layer A is now in the substrate; demotion would consume it. Layer B remains deferred until §2.6 operationalization.
    - **Merge / split / dissolution lifecycle events** — per Charter §2.5, each lands as a separate Cat I record type. Merge MUST reference all antecedent hypotheses (cross-subtype merge per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) produces a typed output record). Split MUST reference the antecedent + each successor.
    - **Other Cat III subtypes' lifecycle events** — `CoordinationRing` / `CampaignHypothesis` / `AutomationGroup` per [`§0010`](#0010--q2-resolution-category-iii-hypothesis-decomposes-into-four-concrete-subtypes-behavioralcluster-coordinationring-campaignhypothesis-automationgroup) each carry their own 6-operation lifecycle. Deferred.
    - **Projection over the lifecycle event chain** — replaying formation + promotion + (future) demotion events to materialize "current hypothesis state" (lifecycle position, promoted-yes/no, cadence params, etc.). Projection layer remains deferred per §2.5 BC3.
    - **Multi-binary consolidation** — `cmd/hypothesis-lifecycle/` with `form` / `promote` / `demote` / `merge` / `split` / `dissolve` subcommands. Five lifecycle ops × four subtypes = 20 potential binaries under the current pattern; consolidation is a cleanup follow-on if/when the binary count bites.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) Cat III pathway with the second lifecycle operation; no prior decision reversed. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.

## `0047` — Third Category III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterDemotion`) lands; promote/demote loop closed; §0011 Layer A cadence gate operationally surfaced

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) materialized the `cadence_seconds` Layer A parameter per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) on every promotion event, but the parameter was structurally underconstrained: no consumer could act on it. Per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) Q4 resolution, demotion is the operation that consumes the cadence gate; without demotion in the substrate, Layer A is recorded but never evaluated. This entry lands `BehavioralClusterDemotion`, closing the promote/demote loop that is the meaningful operational cycle per Charter §1, and operationally surfaces the Layer A cadence gate via a `cadence_satisfied` report field.

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralClusterDemotion` as the third Cat III lifecycle event, with three structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterDemotion` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_demotion.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_demotion.proto). Three fields:

     - `promotion_event_hash` (bytes) — content-hash of the `BehavioralClusterPromotion` event being ended. Per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) multiple promotions can exist for the same formation under different cadence parameters, so demotion references the PROMOTION (not the formation) to identify which cadence gate is closing.
     - `demoted_at` (int64 Unix ns) — when demotion was recorded. The cadence-gate state is computed by consumers as `(demoted_at - promotion.promoted_at) >= promotion.cadence_seconds * 1e9`.
     - `reason` (string, optional) — operator forensic note; strongly recommended when demoting within the cadence window.

     No denormalized `formation_event_hash` field per Charter §2.5 BC3 (forbids storing "current state" alongside lifecycle events; the projection layer resolves the chain via two-hop lookup `demotion → promotion → formation`).

  2. **`hypothesis.Demote(ctx, sub, opts, now)` entry point** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion.go). Validates target hash resolves to a `BehavioralClusterPromotion` row (returns `ErrTargetNotFound` or `ErrTargetWrongType` — the §2.5-lifecycle-integrity errors — when the predecessor is missing or mis-typed). Computes `cadence_satisfied` + `cadence_elapsed_seconds` from the source promotion's `promoted_at` + `cadence_seconds`; **records the demotion REGARDLESS of cadence-gate state**.

     Cadence-gate semantics per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred): Layer A is a CANDIDACY criterion, not a hard barrier. The Charter does not say "demotion REQUIRES Layer A satisfied"; it says "demotion BECOMES A CANDIDATE when Layer A holds." Operators may demote within the cadence window (Layer A unsatisfied) when operational urgency or future Layer B criteria justify; the substrate records the discrete event + the report surfaces the gate state. Layer B (deep criterion on evidential independence) remains deferred until [§2.6](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#26-evidential-independence-integrity) operationalization; Demote does not evaluate it.

  3. **Shared error renamed** — `ErrTargetNotFound` + `ErrTargetWrongType` (originally introduced at [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) for `Promote`) generalized to operation-agnostic messages so they apply to both `Promote` (expects formation predecessor) and `Demote` (expects promotion predecessor). Backward-equivalent for `Promote`'s callers; sentinel-error identity preserved.

  Operator interface: [`cmd/demote-hypothesis`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/demote-hypothesis/main.go). Sixth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`. Command-line options: `-promotion-event-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-demoted-at-ns` (default 0 = wall-clock now), `-reason` (optional). Structured JSON output includes `cadence_satisfied` + `cadence_elapsed_seconds` for operator-facing visibility. Exit codes: **0** success, **2** tool/configuration error, **3** target-not-found or target-wrong-type.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterDemotion` committed under content-addressed immutability; no prior lifecycle event mutated. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — demotion is a Cat III lifecycle operation; type-distinct from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — third of six lifecycle operations recorded as a discrete immutable event. Respects §2.5 BC3 — no denormalized hypothesis-state field; chain reconstruction via projection (two-hop `demotion → promotion → formation` lookup). Respects §2.5 BC5 — the demotion event IS a Cat I record under §2.1. Respects [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred) — Layer A treated as candidacy gate (informational), not barrier; Layer B explicitly deferred to §2.6. Respects [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) — demotion consumes the promotion's cadence parameter; predecessor relationship is structural (typed reference + validation). Canonical vocabulary used as written: demotion, promotion, lifecycle event, hypothesis, substrate, cadence.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_demotion.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_demotion.proto) — new Cat III lifecycle event. Three fields; constitutional anchors in the file header.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `demote-hypothesis-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion.go) — new file. `DemoteOptions`, `DemoteReport` (with `CadenceSatisfied` + `CadenceElapsedSeconds` flags), `Demote` entry point.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/promotion.go) — `ErrTargetNotFound` + `ErrTargetWrongType` message strings generalized (no callers depended on the prior strings; `errors.Is(err, ErrTargetNotFound)` identity preserved).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/demotion_test.go) — 9 tests covering full promote/demote loop, cadence-satisfied true/false/exact-boundary, idempotency, versioning, unknown target, wrong-type target (formation hash), default `demoted_at`, full-chain-in-substrate (7-row count check).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/demote-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/demote-hypothesis/main.go) — new binary; sixth operational CLI.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIFullLoop` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing formation + promotion + demotion lifecycle events; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across all three Cat III operations).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-demotion"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-demotion-{minimal,typical}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files**. Mirrors the established Cat III coverage shape.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status + entries table updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`demote-hypothesis` CLI section with cadence-gate-semantics explainer.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0047 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **164 tests** across the service (up from 154 at [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable); +10 = 9 demotion + 1 verify-full-loop + 2 corpus subtests + helpers). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Sixth operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains six binaries covering audit (`verify`), maintenance (`orphan-cleanup`), Cat II derivation (`derive-operational-session`), Cat III formation (`form-hypothesis`), Cat III promotion (`promote-hypothesis`), Cat III demotion (`demote-hypothesis`).
  - **§0011 Layer A cadence gate now operationally surfaced.** Every demotion event commits with a structured report indicating whether the gate was satisfied at the moment of demotion. The cadence parameter is no longer write-only — it is now consumed by a producer (Demote) and reported to operators.
  - **Promote/demote loop closed.** The Cat III lifecycle now has the operational cycle most meaningful per Charter §1: hypothesis forms → promotes into operational use → demotes out of operational use. Merge, split, and dissolution remain follow-on landings (3 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable).
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/demote-hypothesis` without `-promotion-event-hash` exits 2; (b) `bin/demote-hypothesis -promotion-event-hash 00...00 -db /tmp/empty.db` exits 3 with `ErrTargetNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Dissolution lifecycle event** (`BehavioralClusterDissolution`) — per Charter §2.5 + glossary, dissolution recognizes that the hypothesis no longer corresponds to any underlying phenomenon (distinguished from demotion: demotion withdraws operational use; dissolution recognizes non-existence). Natural fourth lifecycle landing.
    - **Merge / split lifecycle events** — per Charter §2.5, merge references all antecedent hypotheses; split references the antecedent + each successor. Cross-subtype merge per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) produces a typed output record; resolution deferred to `lifecycle-semantics.md` post-Q4 redaction.
    - **Layer B deep-criterion** — per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred), the structural test on evidential independence that completes the staged-combination criterion. Deferred until §2.6 operationalization; once §2.6 redacts, demotion may optionally enforce both Layer A + Layer B AND-composition before recording (currently neither layer is enforced).
    - **Re-promotion semantics** — operator demotes a hypothesis, then later promotes it again. Two distinct promotion events exist in the substrate, each with its own (cadence_seconds, reason). The substrate accepts both; projection-layer + operator-facing reporting needs to surface the multi-promotion lifecycle (current state inference is deferred along with projection layer).
    - **Projection over the full lifecycle event chain** — read-side materialization of "current hypothesis state" (lifecycle position, promoted/demoted status, cadence params, time-since-last-event, etc.) by replaying formation + promotion + demotion + (future) merge/split/dissolution events. Projection layer remains deferred per Charter §2.5 BC3.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) Cat III pathway with the third lifecycle operation; no prior decision reversed. The generalization of `ErrTargetNotFound` + `ErrTargetWrongType` error strings is a non-amending refinement (sentinel identity preserved; backward-equivalent for `Promote`'s callers). [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.
