## `0048` — Fourth Category III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterDissolution`) lands; demotion/dissolution distinction operationally surfaced; 4 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced) closed the promote/demote loop and named dissolution as the natural fourth lifecycle landing. The glossary + [`lifecycle-semantics.md` line 36](../ontology/lifecycle-semantics.md) draw a constitutional distinction: demotion withdraws OPERATIONAL USE; dissolution recognizes NON-EXISTENCE of the underlying phenomenon. Until this entry the distinction was canonically stated but not operationally observable — no producer existed for a discrete dissolution event, so a substrate could not exhibit the two operations side-by-side. This entry lands `BehavioralClusterDissolution`, making the demotion/dissolution distinction structurally observable rather than merely lexical.

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralClusterDissolution` as the fourth Cat III lifecycle event, with three structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterDissolution` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_dissolution.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_dissolution.proto). Three fields:

     - `formation_event_hash` (bytes) — content-hash of the `BehavioralClusterFormation` event identifying the hypothesis being dissolved. Per [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) hypothesis identity IS the formation event's content-hash; dissolution targets the formation DIRECTLY (no two-hop indirection through promotion, unlike demotion). This is the structural manifestation of the demotion/dissolution distinction: demotion targets a specific promotion (which cadence gate is closing); dissolution targets the hypothesis itself (which phenomenon claim is being retracted).
     - `dissolved_at` (int64 Unix ns) — when dissolution was recorded.
     - `reason` (string, optional) — operator forensic note; strongly recommended (dissolution is the terminal lifecycle operation on a hypothesis and the absence of a reason removes the only record of the underlying judgment).

     No denormalized "currently_promoted" or "promotion_event_hash" field per Charter §2.5 BC3 (forbids storing "current state" alongside lifecycle events; the projection layer resolves the chain).

  2. **`hypothesis.Dissolve(ctx, sub, opts, now)` entry point** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution.go). Validates target hash resolves to a `BehavioralClusterFormation` row (returns `ErrTargetNotFound` or `ErrTargetWrongType` — the §2.5-lifecycle-integrity errors — when the predecessor is missing or mis-typed). No cadence-gate semantics — dissolution is not subject to Layer A / Layer B candidacy gates per [`§0011`](#0011--q4-resolution-staged-combination-of-cadence-layer-a--deep-criterion-layer-b-for-demotion-candidacy-layer-b-deferred), which apply only to demotion-candidacy.

     Order-agnostic per substrate immutability: dissolution may be invoked whether the hypothesis was ever promoted or not. A formation that was never admitted to operational use can still be recognized as not corresponding to any phenomenon; the substrate accepts the discrete lifecycle event regardless of intermediate state. The projection layer reconstructs the full chain per §2.5 BC3.

  3. **Predecessor-type validation reuses the shared sentinel errors** introduced at [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) and generalized at [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced) — `ErrTargetNotFound` + `ErrTargetWrongType` are now consumed by three lifecycle operations (Promote, Demote, Dissolve). Their operation-agnostic message form (per §0047) accommodates all three callers without further change.

  Operator interface: [`cmd/dissolve-hypothesis`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/dissolve-hypothesis/main.go). Seventh operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`, `demote-hypothesis`. Command-line options: `-formation-event-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-dissolved-at-ns` (default 0 = wall-clock now), `-reason` (optional). Structured JSON output: `formation_event_hash`, `dissolution_event_hash`, `already_dissolved`. Exit codes: **0** success, **2** tool/configuration error, **3** target-not-found or target-wrong-type.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterDissolution` committed under content-addressed immutability; no prior lifecycle event mutated. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — dissolution is a Cat III lifecycle operation; type-distinct from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — fourth of six lifecycle operations recorded as a discrete immutable event. Respects §2.5 BC3 — no denormalized hypothesis-state field; chain reconstruction via projection. Respects §2.5 BC5 — the dissolution event IS a Cat I record under §2.1. Respects glossary + [`lifecycle-semantics.md` line 36](../ontology/lifecycle-semantics.md) — dissolution distinguished from demotion by referent (formation vs promotion) and by semantic claim (non-existence vs operational withdrawal); the two operations are not interchangeable and the substrate now exhibits both as discrete event types. Canonical vocabulary used as written: dissolution, demotion, promotion, formation, lifecycle event, hypothesis, substrate.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_dissolution.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_dissolution.proto) — new Cat III lifecycle event. Three fields; constitutional anchors in the file header citing the demotion/dissolution distinction.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `dissolve-hypothesis-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution.go) — new file. `DissolveOptions`, `DissolveReport`, `Dissolve` entry point. Reuses the `behavioralClusterFormationMessageType` constant + the shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/dissolution_test.go) — **8 tests** covering direct-from-formation dissolution, dissolution after promote/demote, idempotency, versioning, unknown target, wrong-type target (promotion hash), default `dissolved_at`, full-lifecycle-in-substrate (8-row count check across all four lifecycle operations + the observation prerequisites).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/dissolve-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/dissolve-hypothesis/main.go) — new binary; seventh operational CLI.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIFullLifecycle` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing formation + promotion + demotion + dissolution lifecycle events; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across all four Cat III lifecycle operations).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-dissolution"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-dissolution-{minimal,typical}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files**. Mirrors the established Cat III coverage shape.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status + entries table updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`dissolve-hypothesis` CLI section with the dissolution/demotion semantic distinction surfaced for operators.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0048 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **177 tests** across the service (up from 164 at [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced); +13 = 8 dissolution + 1 verify-full-lifecycle + 2 corpus subtests + helpers). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Seventh operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains seven binaries covering audit (`verify`), maintenance (`orphan-cleanup`), Cat II derivation (`derive-operational-session`), Cat III formation (`form-hypothesis`), Cat III promotion (`promote-hypothesis`), Cat III demotion (`demote-hypothesis`), Cat III dissolution (`dissolve-hypothesis`).
  - **Demotion/dissolution distinction now operationally observable.** A substrate may now carry both a `BehavioralClusterDemotion` row and a `BehavioralClusterDissolution` row against the same hypothesis chain — the two are type-distinct, target distinct predecessors (promotion vs formation), and encode distinct semantic claims (operational withdrawal vs phenomenon non-existence). The Charter §2.5 + glossary distinction is no longer purely lexical.
  - **4 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable.** Formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution have producers + tests + corpus entries. Merge and split remain follow-on landings.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/dissolve-hypothesis` without `-formation-event-hash` exits 2; (b) `bin/dissolve-hypothesis -formation-event-hash 00...00 -db /tmp/empty.db` exits 3 with `ErrTargetNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Merge / split lifecycle events** — per Charter §2.5, merge references all antecedent hypotheses; split references the antecedent + each successor. Cross-subtype merge per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) produces a typed output record; resolution deferred to `lifecycle-semantics.md` post-Q4 redaction. With dissolution landed, merge + split are the remaining 2 of 6 lifecycle operations.
    - **Re-formation semantics** — operator dissolves a hypothesis, then later the same observation pattern recurs and a new formation fires under the same signature. Two formation events with identical pattern_signature + actor_refs would collide at content-hash if the source_event_hashes are unchanged; if the source set differs (new observations after dissolution), the second formation event is structurally distinct and the substrate accepts it. Projection-layer + operator-facing reporting needs to surface this multi-formation arc.
    - **Cross-operation ordering constraints** — the current implementation imposes no ordering between dissolution and promotion/demotion. Operators may dissolve a currently-promoted hypothesis without first demoting; the substrate accepts the discrete event. Whether the projection layer should treat dissolution-of-active-promotion as an exceptional or normal case is deferred to projection-layer design.
    - **Layer B deep-criterion** — unchanged from [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced); deferred until §2.6 operationalization.
    - **Projection over the full lifecycle event chain** — unchanged from [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced); read-side materialization remains deferred per Charter §2.5 BC3. With dissolution landed, the projection layer needs to recognize four lifecycle event types when it is eventually built.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced) Cat III pathway with the fourth lifecycle operation; no prior decision reversed. The shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels generalized at §0047 accommodate the third caller (Dissolve) without further change. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.

## `0049` — Fifth Category III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterMerge`) lands; within-subtype binary merge; produced-hypothesis-as-separate-formation structural choice; 5 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0048`](#0048--fourth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdissolution-lands-demotiondissolution-distinction-operationally-surfaced-4-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable) closed the demotion/dissolution distinction and named merge + split as the remaining two-of-six lifecycle operations. The glossary + [`lifecycle-semantics.md` line 28](../ontology/lifecycle-semantics.md) define merge: "Two hypotheses ... are recognized as describing the same underlying phenomenon and combined. Merge is recorded as an immutable event referencing both antecedents and the produced hypothesis." Until this entry the merge operation was canonically stated but not operationally observable — no producer existed for a discrete merge event, so a substrate could not exhibit two formations being recognized as the same phenomenon. This entry lands `BehavioralClusterMerge` (within-subtype only), making the merge lifecycle operation structurally observable.

  The merge lifecycle operation surfaced two structural choices not pre-resolved by the Charter, both decided in this entry against current structural ground:

  - **Cross-subtype scope.** Per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md): "Cross-subtype merge ... requires a typed transformation: the merge operation produces a typed output record. Whether the produced record is a third concrete subtype or an abstract record with subtype-elision is a question whose resolution is deferred to `lifecycle-semantics.md` post-Q4 redaction." Cross-subtype merge typing remains an open Ontology question and is therefore NOT implemented at this layer.
  - **Produced-hypothesis identity.** Lifecycle-semantics line 28 says the merge event references "both antecedents and the produced hypothesis" — three referents, not two. Per [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) hypothesis identity IS the formation event's content-hash. Two coherent options existed: (A) the merge event's own content-hash acts as the new hypothesis identity (collapsing produced hypothesis into the merge event itself); (B) the merge event references a separately-committed new `BehavioralClusterFormation` whose content-hash is the produced hypothesis's identity. Option (A) would violate the §0045 invariant and require every downstream lifecycle operator (`Promote`, `Demote`, `Dissolve`) to handle merge-event hashes as valid targets in addition to formation hashes — a substantial cross-cutting refactor. Option (B) preserves the §0045 invariant: all lifecycle operations continue to target formations uniformly through `behavioralClusterFormationMessageType`. This entry adopts Option (B).

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralClusterMerge` as the fifth Cat III lifecycle event, with four structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterMerge` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_merge.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_merge.proto). Four fields:

     - `antecedent_formation_event_hashes` (repeated bytes) — content-hashes of the two `BehavioralClusterFormation` events being merged. Sorted ASCENDING for content-hash stability — the merge relation is symmetric and the substrate identity MUST NOT depend on caller argument order. Cardinality MUST be exactly 2 at this layer (within-subtype binary merge); the repeated shape leaves room for future n-ary or cross-subtype merge per lifecycle-semantics.md post-Q4 redaction without breaking the wire shape.
     - `produced_formation_event_hash` (bytes) — content-hash of a separately-committed `BehavioralClusterFormation` representing the merged hypothesis (Option B per §Context above). Subsequent lifecycle operations target THIS hash, not the merge event's own hash.
     - `merged_at` (int64 Unix ns) — when the merge recognition was recorded.
     - `reason` (string, optional) — operator forensic note; strongly recommended (merge encodes a substantive epistemic claim — the two hypotheses were the same thing all along — and the absence of a reason removes the only record of the underlying judgment).

     No denormalized "produced_subtype" or "currently_merged" field per Charter §2.5 BC3 (forbids storing "current state" alongside lifecycle events; the projection layer resolves the chain).

  2. **`hypothesis.Merge(ctx, sub, opts, now)` entry point** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge.go). Validates all three target hashes resolve to `BehavioralClusterFormation` rows (returns `ErrTargetNotFound` or `ErrTargetWrongType` — the §2.5-lifecycle-integrity errors — when any predecessor is missing or mis-typed). Validates the two antecedents are byte-distinct (returns the new `ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical` otherwise — per lifecycle-semantics.md line 28 merge combines TWO hypotheses; merging a hypothesis with itself is not a valid §2.5 lifecycle move). Sorts the two antecedent hashes ascending before recording so `Merge(A, B)` and `Merge(B, A)` produce a byte-identical merge event content-hash (symmetric-relation idempotency).

     Order-agnostic per substrate immutability: the substrate accepts the discrete merge event regardless of intermediate state of either antecedent (a previously-dissolved formation may still appear as an antecedent — see test `TestMergeFullLifecycleInSubstrate`). Cross-operation ordering constraints (e.g. should merging a previously-dissolved hypothesis be exceptional?) are deferred to projection-layer design per the §0048 carry-forwards.

  3. **Predecessor-type validation reuses the shared sentinel errors** introduced at [`§0046`](#0046--second-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterpromotion-lands-layer-a-cadence-per-0011-materialized-25-lifecycle-now-plural-observable) and generalized at [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced) — `ErrTargetNotFound` + `ErrTargetWrongType` are now consumed by four lifecycle operations (Promote, Demote, Dissolve, Merge). The new `ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical` sentinel is merge-specific (no other lifecycle operation has the symmetric-binary structural constraint).

  4. **`cmd/merge-hypotheses` operator interface** at [`services/ingestion/cmd/merge-hypotheses/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/merge-hypotheses/main.go). Eighth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`, `demote-hypothesis`, `dissolve-hypothesis`. Command-line options: `-antecedent-a-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-antecedent-b-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-produced-formation-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-merged-at-ns` (default 0 = wall-clock now), `-reason` (optional). Structured JSON output: `antecedent_a_hash`, `antecedent_b_hash`, `produced_formation_hash`, `merge_event_hash`, `already_merged`. Exit codes: **0** success, **2** tool/configuration error, **3** target-not-found OR target-wrong-type OR identical-antecedents.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterMerge` committed under content-addressed immutability; no prior lifecycle event mutated; ascending-sort normalization preserves §2.1 by ensuring symmetric-call idempotency rather than producing distinct rows for semantically-equivalent calls. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — merge is a Cat III lifecycle operation; type-distinct from Cat I observations + Cat II constructs. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — fifth of six lifecycle operations recorded as a discrete immutable event referencing both antecedents AND the produced hypothesis per lifecycle-semantics.md line 28. Respects §2.5 BC3 — no denormalized hypothesis-state field; chain reconstruction via projection. Respects §2.5 BC5 — the merge event IS a Cat I record under §2.1. Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — merge event references both antecedent formations + produced formation by content-hash; the chain is reconstructible. Respects [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) — hypothesis identity IS the formation event's content-hash; the structural choice (Option B per §Context) preserves this invariant by routing the produced hypothesis through a separately-committed formation rather than collapsing identity into the merge event itself. Respects [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) — within-subtype scope at this layer; cross-subtype output-typing remains the Ontology open question per Q4 deferral. Canonical vocabulary used as written: merge, formation, lifecycle event, hypothesis, substrate, BehavioralCluster.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_merge.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_merge.proto) — new Cat III lifecycle event. Four fields; constitutional anchors in the file header citing the produced-hypothesis structural choice + cross-subtype deferral.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `merge-hypotheses-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge.go) — new file. `MergeOptions`, `MergeReport`, `Merge` entry point; new `ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical` sentinel; reuses the shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/merge_test.go) — **9 tests** covering happy-path, argument-order-invariance (symmetric-relation collapse), identical-antecedents rejection, idempotency, versioning, unknown antecedent, unknown produced, wrong-type target (promotion hash as antecedent), default `merged_at`, full-lifecycle-in-substrate (19-row count check across all 5 lifecycle operations + 3 formations + 6 observations + 6 ingestion-event enrichments).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/merge-hypotheses/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/merge-hypotheses/main.go) — new binary; eighth operational CLI.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIFullLifecyclePlusMerge` proves verify reports success over a substrate containing all five Cat III lifecycle event types; the substrate-integrity audit is type-agnostic across all five Cat III lifecycle operations).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-merge"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-merge-{minimal,typical}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files**. Mirrors the established Cat III coverage shape; `typical` exercises both sorted antecedent hashes + the produced formation hash.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status + entries table updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`merge-hypotheses` CLI section with argument-order-invariance + Option-B structural-choice explainer; lifecycle-summary paragraph (under §`form-hypothesis`) updated to 5 of 6 operations landed.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0049 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **190 tests** across the service (up from 177 at [`§0048`](#0048--fourth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdissolution-lands-demotiondissolution-distinction-operationally-surfaced-4-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable); +13 = 9 merge + 1 verify-full-lifecycle-plus-merge + 2 corpus subtests + helpers). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Eighth operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains eight binaries covering audit (`verify`), maintenance (`orphan-cleanup`), Cat II derivation (`derive-operational-session`), Cat III formation (`form-hypothesis`), Cat III promotion (`promote-hypothesis`), Cat III demotion (`demote-hypothesis`), Cat III dissolution (`dissolve-hypothesis`), Cat III merge (`merge-hypotheses`).
  - **§0045 hypothesis-identity invariant preserved across the lifecycle surface.** Per Option B per §Context, all five lifecycle operations (Promote, Demote, Dissolve, Merge antecedents, Merge produced) target hypotheses through `BehavioralClusterFormation` content-hashes uniformly. The merge event records merge-specific provenance (which two prior formations were combined); the produced hypothesis's identity remains its own formation event's content-hash. This invariant uniformity simplifies the future projection layer + the future split operation (which is the structural inverse of merge and will use the same separate-formation pattern).
  - **5 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable.** Formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution, merge have producers + tests + corpus entries. Split remains the final follow-on landing (1 of 6 lifecycle operations outstanding).
  - **Symmetric-relation idempotency formalized.** Merge is the first lifecycle operation with symmetric arguments (both antecedents are interchangeable in the abstract operation). The ascending-sort normalization is a new structural pattern — earlier lifecycle operations (Promote, Demote, Dissolve) had asymmetric referents (formation vs promotion) so order was meaningful. This pattern will recur in split (whose successors are interchangeable as a set, though the antecedent is unique) and may inform future n-ary or cross-subtype merge.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/merge-hypotheses` without `-antecedent-a-hash` exits 2; (b) `bin/merge-hypotheses` with bogus 32-byte hashes against empty substrate exits 3 with `ErrTargetNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Split lifecycle event** — per Charter §2.5, split is the structural inverse of merge: a hypothesis recognized as containing multiple distinct phenomena is divided into multiple successor hypotheses. The successor identity question is symmetric with merge's produced-identity question and will be resolved the same way (each successor is a separately-committed `BehavioralClusterFormation`). Final 1 of 6 lifecycle operations.
    - **Cross-subtype merge** — per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md), the typing of the produced record when merging across subtype boundaries (e.g. `BehavioralCluster` ⊕ `CoordinationRing`) is deferred to `lifecycle-semantics.md` post-Q4 redaction. The current proto's `repeated bytes antecedent_formation_event_hashes` wire shape accommodates future n-ary cross-subtype merge without amendment; only producer-side validation tightens to length=2 + same-subtype at this layer.
    - **N-ary merge** — three or more antecedents recognized as the same phenomenon simultaneously. Structurally permissible per the `repeated bytes` proto shape but operationally undefined; deferred along with cross-subtype merge.
    - **Structural-consistency validation between produced and antecedents** — at this layer the substrate does NOT enforce that the produced formation's `source_event_hashes` ⊇ union of antecedents' source hashes, or that its `actor_refs` ⊇ union of antecedents' actor sets. Those are operational conventions of the inference process producing the merge; verifiable at projection time but not gated at write time. Verifying the consistency claim at write time is deferred until the projection layer reveals the audit shape that operators want.
    - **Re-formation semantics + Re-promotion semantics** — unchanged from [`§0048`](#0048--fourth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdissolution-lands-demotiondissolution-distinction-operationally-surfaced-4-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable) carry-forwards.
    - **Layer B deep-criterion** — unchanged from [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced); deferred until §2.6 operationalization.
    - **Projection over the full lifecycle event chain** — unchanged from [`§0048`](#0048--fourth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdissolution-lands-demotiondissolution-distinction-operationally-surfaced-4-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable); read-side materialization remains deferred per Charter §2.5 BC3. With merge landed, the projection layer needs to recognize five lifecycle event types + reconstruct merge-graph topology when it is eventually built.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0048`](#0048--fourth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdissolution-lands-demotiondissolution-distinction-operationally-surfaced-4-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable) Cat III pathway with the fifth lifecycle operation; no prior decision reversed. The shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels generalized at §0047 accommodate the fourth caller (Merge) without further change. The new `ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical` sentinel is merge-specific and does not affect other lifecycle operators. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.

---

## `0050` — Sixth (final) Category III lifecycle event (`BehavioralClusterSplit`) lands; structural inverse of merge; 6 of 6 lifecycle operations now structurally observable; §2.5 lifecycle surface complete

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0049`](#0049--fifth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustermerge-lands-within-subtype-binary-merge-produced-hypothesis-as-separate-formation-structural-choice-5-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable) landed merge as the fifth lifecycle operation and named split as the final follow-on. The glossary + [`lifecycle-semantics.md` line 29](../ontology/lifecycle-semantics.md) define split: "A hypothesis is recognized as containing multiple distinct phenomena and is divided into multiple successor hypotheses. Recorded as an immutable event referencing the antecedent and each successor." Split is the structural inverse of merge per §0049 §Context (merge: 2-to-1, split: 1-to-N). This entry lands `BehavioralClusterSplit` (within-subtype only), completing the §2.5 lifecycle surface: all six lifecycle operations defined at the abstract `Hypothesis` type level (per [`entity-model.md` Category III](../ontology/entity-model.md)) are now operationally observable for the BehavioralCluster concrete subtype.

  The structural choices made in §0049 §Context for merge transfer mechanically to split with one adaptation:

  - **Cross-subtype scope.** Per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) the same Q4 deferral applies: whether a cross-subtype split (one BehavioralCluster recognized as containing distinct BehavioralCluster + CoordinationRing phenomena) produces successors of mixed types, of a single uniform abstract type, or some other shape remains an open Ontology question. Cross-subtype split is NOT implemented at this layer.
  - **Successor identity (mirrors §0049 Option B).** Each successor is a separately-committed `BehavioralClusterFormation` whose content-hash is the successor hypothesis's identity per [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable). The split event references the antecedent formation hash + each successor formation hash. This preserves the §0045 invariant uniformly across all six lifecycle operations: every lifecycle operator (Promote, Demote, Dissolve, Merge antecedents, Merge produced, Split antecedent, Split successors) reaches a hypothesis via its `BehavioralClusterFormation` content-hash. The same Option (A) rejected at §0049 (collapse the successor identities into the split event itself) is rejected here for the same reason: it would violate §0045 and require cross-cutting refactor of every downstream operator.
  - **Successor cardinality.** Per lifecycle-semantics.md line 29 ("multiple successor hypotheses") the canonical definition requires at least two successors. Length-1 "split" is operationally indistinguishable from a no-op rename (substrate's content-addressing already prevents that), so length ≥ 2 is enforced as a structural precondition. The proto's `repeated bytes` shape accommodates future n-ary or cross-subtype split without amendment.
  - **Successor distinctness (asymmetric extension vs merge).** Merge required the two antecedents to be byte-distinct (`ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical`). Split requires a stronger condition: every successor MUST be byte-distinct from every other successor AND from the antecedent. Mathematically: the successor set partitions the antecedent's underlying phenomena; the antecedent's own identity cannot reappear in its own partition (otherwise nothing was split). The new `ErrSplitSuccessorsNotDistinct` sentinel covers both internal-duplicates and antecedent-overlap with a single error class — the wrong-shape condition is the same (successors-not-distinct-from-the-relevant-set), even if the offending hash differs.

- **Decision:** Land `BehavioralClusterSplit` as the sixth (and final) Cat III lifecycle event, with four structural moves:

  1. **`BehavioralClusterSplit` Protobuf message** at [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_split.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_split.proto). Four fields:

     - `antecedent_formation_event_hash` (bytes) — single antecedent formation hash. Asymmetric shape vs merge's two-antecedent symmetry (merge is 2-to-1, split is 1-to-N).
     - `successor_formation_event_hashes` (repeated bytes) — content-hashes of the separately-committed successor `BehavioralClusterFormation` events. Sorted ASCENDING for content-hash stability (successors form a SET, not a sequence). Cardinality MUST be ≥ 2 at this layer; all entries MUST be byte-distinct from each other AND from `antecedent_formation_event_hash`.
     - `split_at` (int64 Unix ns) — when the split recognition was recorded.
     - `reason` (string, optional) — operator forensic note; strongly recommended.

     No denormalized "successor_count" or "currently_split" field per Charter §2.5 BC3.

  2. **`hypothesis.Split(ctx, sub, opts, now)` entry point** at [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split.go). Validates `len(successors) >= 2` (returns the new `ErrSplitInsufficientSuccessors`); validates the successor set is byte-distinct and disjoint from `{antecedent}` (returns the new `ErrSplitSuccessorsNotDistinct`); validates antecedent + every successor resolves to `BehavioralClusterFormation` (reuses the shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels — fifth caller). Sorts successors ascending before recording so the split event's content-hash is invariant under successor enumeration order (set-equality idempotency).

     Order-agnostic per substrate immutability: the substrate accepts the discrete split event regardless of intermediate state of any referenced formation (a previously-dissolved or previously-merged formation may still appear as antecedent or successor — see test `TestSplitAllSixLifecycleOpsInSubstrate`). Cross-operation ordering constraints (should splitting a dissolved hypothesis be exceptional? should the successors typically be freshly-formed?) are operational conventions of the inference process producing the split, deferred to projection-layer design per the §0048/§0049 carry-forwards.

  3. **Sentinel-error landscape closure.** Five sentinels now suffice for the six lifecycle operations:
     - `ErrTargetNotFound` (§0046) — used by Promote, Demote, Dissolve, Merge, Split (all five §2.5-mediating operations).
     - `ErrTargetWrongType` (§0046) — used by the same five operations.
     - `ErrMergeAntecedentsIdentical` (§0049) — merge-specific (two-antecedent symmetry).
     - `ErrSplitInsufficientSuccessors` (this entry) — split-specific (cardinality precondition).
     - `ErrSplitSuccessorsNotDistinct` (this entry) — split-specific (set-distinctness precondition).

     No further sentinel proliferation is anticipated for projection-layer or future cross-subtype work; if it surfaces it will arrive with a follow-on entry against the new structural ground.

  4. **`cmd/split-hypothesis` operator interface** at [`services/ingestion/cmd/split-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/split-hypothesis/main.go). Ninth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`, `demote-hypothesis`, `dissolve-hypothesis`, `merge-hypotheses`. Command-line options: `-antecedent-formation-hash` (required, 64-char hex), `-successor-formation-hash` (required, **repeatable**, ≥ 2 invocations; 64-char hex each), `-split-at-ns` (default 0 = wall-clock now), `-reason` (optional). The repeatable successor option uses a custom `successorList` accumulator implementing the Go-standard option-parser interface. Structured JSON output: `antecedent_formation_hash`, `successor_formation_hashes`, `split_event_hash`, `already_split`. Exit codes: **0** success, **2** tool/configuration error, **3** target-not-found OR target-wrong-type OR insufficient-successors OR successors-not-distinct.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — `BehavioralClusterSplit` committed under content-addressed immutability; ascending-sort normalization preserves §2.1 by ensuring set-equality idempotency. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — split is a Cat III lifecycle operation; type-distinct from Cat I + Cat II. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — sixth (and final) of six lifecycle operations recorded as discrete immutable event referencing the antecedent AND each successor per lifecycle-semantics.md line 29. Respects §2.5 BC3 — no denormalized hypothesis-state field. Respects §2.5 BC5 — the split event IS a Cat I record. Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — split event references antecedent + every successor by content-hash; the chain is reconstructible. Respects [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) — hypothesis identity preserved; the Option-B-mirror structural choice routes successor identities through separately-committed formations rather than collapsing into the split event itself. Respects [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md) — within-subtype scope at this layer; cross-subtype successor-typing remains the Ontology open question per Q4 deferral. Canonical vocabulary used as written: split, formation, lifecycle event, hypothesis, substrate, BehavioralCluster.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_split.proto`](../../schemas/events/v1/behavioral_cluster_split.proto) — new (and final) Cat III lifecycle event. Four fields; constitutional anchors in the file header citing the inverse-of-merge relationship + cross-subtype deferral.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — generate target extended; new `split-hypothesis-build` target.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split.go) — new file. `SplitOptions`, `SplitReport`, `Split` entry point; two new sentinels (`ErrSplitInsufficientSuccessors`, `ErrSplitSuccessorsNotDistinct`); reuses the shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels (fifth caller).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/hypothesis/split_test.go) — **11 tests** covering happy-path, successor-order invariance (set-equality collapse), insufficient successors, duplicate successors, antecedent-equals-successor, idempotency, versioning, unknown antecedent, unknown successor, wrong-type target (promotion hash as successor), default `split_at`, all-six-lifecycle-ops-in-substrate (20-row count check across the complete §2.5 lifecycle surface + 3 formations + 6 observations + 6 ingestion-event enrichments).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/split-hypothesis/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/split-hypothesis/main.go) — new binary; ninth operational CLI; accepts the `-successor-formation-hash` option repeatedly to accumulate the successor hash set.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/verify/verify_test.go) — **1 new test** (`TestVerifyWithCatIIIAllSixLifecycleOps` — terminal end-to-end coverage proving verify reports success over a substrate containing all six Cat III lifecycle event types).
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/corpus_test.go) — `messageFactory` extended with `"behavioral-cluster-split"`.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/behavioral-cluster-split-{minimal,typical}.{json,bin,hash}`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/) — **6 new corpus files**. Mirrors the established Cat III coverage shape; `typical` exercises the antecedent hash + two sorted successor hashes.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/internal/canonical/testdata/canonical-corpus/README.md) — Status + entries table updated to record §2.5 lifecycle surface completion.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`split-hypothesis` CLI section with successor-order-invariance + inverse-of-merge explainer; lifecycle-summary paragraph updated to 6 of 6 landed.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0050 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **205 tests** across the service (up from 190 at [`§0049`](#0049--fifth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustermerge-lands-within-subtype-binary-merge-produced-hypothesis-as-separate-formation-structural-choice-5-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable); +15 = 11 split + 1 verify-all-six + 2 corpus subtests + helpers). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Ninth operational binary lands.** `cmd/` now contains nine binaries covering audit (`verify`), maintenance (`orphan-cleanup`), Cat II derivation (`derive-operational-session`), and all six Cat III lifecycle operations (`form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`, `demote-hypothesis`, `dissolve-hypothesis`, `merge-hypotheses`, `split-hypothesis`).
  - **§2.5 lifecycle surface complete.** All six lifecycle operations defined at the abstract `Hypothesis` type level per [`entity-model.md` Category III](../ontology/entity-model.md) — formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution, merge, split — are now operationally observable for the `BehavioralCluster` concrete subtype with producers + tests + corpus entries + CLI binaries. This closes the lifecycle-landing arc opened at [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) and extended sequentially through §0046 (promotion), §0047 (demotion), §0048 (dissolution), §0049 (merge), §0050 (split — this entry).
  - **§0045 invariant uniformly preserved.** All five lifecycle operators (Promote, Demote, Dissolve, Merge, Split) reference hypotheses through `BehavioralClusterFormation` content-hashes. The Option-B pattern (separately-committed formation for produced/successor identities) introduced at §0049 transfers to split without modification. Future projection-layer work can rely on this uniformity: traversing the lifecycle graph means following formation-hash references.
  - **Set-equality idempotency formalized as the second normalization pattern.** Merge introduced ascending-sort normalization for symmetric binary arguments (§0049); split extends it to symmetric n-ary set arguments. The pattern is now mature: any lifecycle operation whose arguments form a SET (interchangeable enumeration) MUST normalize via ascending-sort before recording so the substrate identity is set-equality-stable. Re-applicable to any future cross-subtype or n-ary lifecycle work.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/split-hypothesis` without `-antecedent-formation-hash` exits 2; (b) `bin/split-hypothesis` with bogus 32-byte antecedent + two bogus successors against empty substrate exits 3 with `ErrTargetNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards — substantially reduced now that the lifecycle surface is complete).**
    - **Cross-subtype lifecycle operations** — per [`entity-model.md` §Cross-subtype operations](../ontology/entity-model.md), the typing of produced (merge) or successor (split) records when crossing subtype boundaries remains the open Ontology question per Q4 deferral. The current proto wire shapes (`repeated bytes` for both merge antecedents and split successors) accommodate future cross-subtype work without amendment; only producer-side validation will tighten or relax when Q4 redacts.
    - **N-ary merge / N-ary split with ≥ 3 elements** — structurally permissible per the `repeated bytes` proto shapes but operationally undefined; deferred along with cross-subtype.
    - **Structural-consistency validation between antecedent and successors** — at this layer the substrate does NOT enforce that the union of successors' `source_event_hashes` ⊇ antecedent's source hashes, or that the successors' `actor_refs` partition the antecedent's. Verifiable at projection time but not gated at write time.
    - **Other concrete Cat III subtypes** — `AutomationGroup`, `CoordinationRing`, `CampaignHypothesis` per [`entity-model.md` Category III](../ontology/entity-model.md) remain unlanded at the implementation layer. The lifecycle-landing arc proven for `BehavioralCluster` (six entries §0045–§0050) is the template; subsequent subtype landings will pursue their own subtype-specific formation patterns + lifecycle event types, NOT a generic abstract surface (per [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) commitment to typed subtype-specific landings rather than uniform-shape Cat III genericity).
    - **Layer B deep-criterion** — unchanged from [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced); deferred until §2.6 operationalization.
    - **Projection over the full lifecycle event chain** — unchanged from [`§0049`](#0049--fifth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustermerge-lands-within-subtype-binary-merge-produced-hypothesis-as-separate-formation-structural-choice-5-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable). With all six lifecycle event types landed, the projection layer now has its complete input surface; the substrate side of §2.5 is no longer the blocker.
    - **Re-formation / Re-promotion / Cross-operation ordering** — unchanged from §0048 carry-forwards.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry extends the [`§0049`](#0049--fifth-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustermerge-lands-within-subtype-binary-merge-produced-hypothesis-as-separate-formation-structural-choice-5-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable) Cat III pathway with the sixth (and final) lifecycle operation; no prior decision reversed. The shared `ErrTargetNotFound` / `ErrTargetWrongType` sentinels generalized at §0047 accommodate the fifth caller (Split) without further change. Two new split-specific sentinels (`ErrSplitInsufficientSuccessors`, `ErrSplitSuccessorsNotDistinct`) close the sentinel landscape for the §2.5 lifecycle surface. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.

## `0051` — First projection-layer landing (`HypothesisProjection`); single-hypothesis read-side materialization over the §2.5 lifecycle event chain; Charter §2.5 BC3 deferred-state read shape now operationally observable

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0050`](#0050--sixth-final-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustersplit-lands-structural-inverse-of-merge-6-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable-25-lifecycle-surface-complete) completed the §2.5 lifecycle surface — six concrete lifecycle event types (formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution, merge, split) are now writable. Every entry in the §0045–§0050 arc carried a Consequences carry-forward naming **"Projection over the full lifecycle event chain"** as deferred per Charter §2.5 BC3 (which forbids storing "current state" as a substrate row — the substrate stores immutable lifecycle events; current state must be reconstructed by replaying them). With the substrate-write side complete, projection is no longer blocked on missing input. This entry lands the first projection-layer surface: single-hypothesis on-demand read-side materialization.

  The projection layer surfaced three structural choices decided in this entry against current ground:

  - **On-demand vs materialized.** Option (A): on-demand walk via `substrate.WalkEvents` per call. Option (B): materialized projection table maintained as a side-effect of substrate writes. Option (A) chosen at this layer per inception-phase substrate sizes (linear walk is acceptable cost) AND the constitutional-minimalism principle (CLAUDE.md §7 — ceremony without behavioral consequence is rejected): a materialized projection table is additional state to invalidate, backup, and verify; the read-side cost of the linear walk does not yet justify that complexity. A follow-on landing may introduce projection-side indexing if the linear walk becomes a bottleneck — that landing will arrive against the new operational ground rather than be predicted now.

  - **Layering: read-only vs read-write coupling.** Option (A): `internal/projection` depends only on `substrate` (for the walk) + `genproto/events/v1` (for unmarshaling lifecycle payloads). Option (B): `internal/projection` reuses lifecycle event constants + helpers from `internal/hypothesis`. Option (A) chosen: projection is read-only; coupling it to the writer surface (`hypothesis` package) would invite circular dependencies as projection-driven inference work lands. The five lifecycle-event message-type discriminator constants are duplicated in `projection.go` rather than imported from `hypothesis.go`; the duplication is structural (3 lines × 5 constants) and acceptable per constitutional-minimalism — drift is detected at compile time the first time either side updates a constant without the other.

  - **State precedence rules.** The projection MUST collapse the per-formation lifecycle history into a single `State` enum value for operator-facing reporting. Precedence chosen (highest → lowest): Dissolved beats everything (terminal per §0048); SplitInto beats MergedInto when both apply (split is the terminal partition); MergedInto beats the promote/demote arc; within promote/demote, Demoted when a demotion targets the latest promotion, otherwise Promoted; default Forming. The precedence is an operational convention of the projection, NOT a Charter invariant — a future revision may redact it. The chosen ordering matches the read shape operators are most likely to want (terminal states surface; in-flight states are visible via the full `LifecycleHistory` regardless of the collapsed `State`).

- **Decision:** Land `HypothesisProjection` as the first projection-layer surface, with four structural moves:

  1. **`internal/projection` package** at [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection.go). New package; not yet a stable read-side API — the inception-phase posture per [`§0027`](#0027--substrate-technology-protocol-buffer-canonical-substrate-on-sqlite-blob-store-third-technology-rfc-per-0022-pivot) applies. Types:

     - `State` (string enum): `forming`, `promoted`, `demoted`, `dissolved`, `merged_into`, `split_into`.
     - `LifecycleEntry`: typed record of one lifecycle event reaching the projected formation (Type = Protobuf descriptor full name, EventHash, EventTime).
     - `HypothesisProjection`: materialized view containing `FormationHash`, computed `State`, and per-lifecycle-type pointers (`LatestPromotion`, `LatestDemotion`, `Dissolution`, `MergedInto`, `SplitInto`) for operator inspection + full chronological `LifecycleHistory`.

     Message-type discriminators duplicated as package-level constants per the layering choice above.

  2. **`ProjectHypothesis(ctx, sub, formationHash)` entry point** at the same file. Two-pass walk:
     - Pass one: collect every promotion event targeting the formation (needed to resolve demotions — demotion targets a promotion hash, not the formation directly).
     - Pass two: collect demotions targeting any promotion of this formation; dissolutions targeting the formation; merges containing the formation in their antecedent set; splits whose antecedent IS the formation.
     - Sort `LifecycleHistory` ascending by `EventTime`; compute terminal `State` per the precedence rules above.

     Errors:
     - `ErrFormationNotFound`: target hash does not resolve to any substrate row.
     - `ErrTargetNotFormation`: target hash resolves to a row whose `message_type` is not `BehavioralClusterFormation` (parallel to `hypothesis.ErrTargetWrongType` but read-side; the projection layer surfaces its own sentinels to maintain layering).

  3. **`cmd/hypothesis-state` operator interface** at [`services/ingestion/cmd/hypothesis-state/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/hypothesis-state/main.go). Tenth operational binary alongside `verify`, `orphan-cleanup`, `derive-operational-session`, `form-hypothesis`, `promote-hypothesis`, `demote-hypothesis`, `dissolve-hypothesis`, `merge-hypotheses`, `split-hypothesis`. First binary in the **read-only** classification — every prior binary either commits substrate rows (`form-hypothesis`, the five lifecycle operators), audits the substrate's hash-chain consistency or maintains the blob-store (`verify`, `orphan-cleanup`), or derives Category II (`derive-operational-session`). Options: `-formation-event-hash` (required, 64-char hex). Structured JSON output describing the full projection. Exit codes: **0** success; **2** tool/configuration error; **3** formation-not-found or target-not-formation.

  4. **Test coverage** at [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection_test.go). 11 tests covering each terminal state (forming, promoted, demoted, dissolved, merged-into, split-into) plus precedence edges (dissolution-beats-promotion, split-beats-merge-against-different-formation), re-promotion arc returning to promoted, lifecycle-history chronological ordering, unknown-formation rejection, wrong-type-target rejection.

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — the projection layer is strictly read-only over the immutable substrate; no lifecycle event is created, modified, or interpreted in a way that would constitute a §2.1 violation. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — the projection's `State` field is an operational summary of the lifecycle chain, NOT a category claim about the underlying phenomenon (the phenomenon claim lives in the formation event itself; the projection reports lifecycle position). Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — every projection entry references its producing lifecycle event by content-hash; the projection is reconstructible from the same substrate state regardless of when or where it is computed. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — projects exactly the six lifecycle operations defined at the abstract `Hypothesis` type level. Respects [§2.5 BC3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — explicitly implements the BC3-mandated read shape ("the hypothesis's current state is a projection over the operation event history") as a discrete operationally-observable surface, completing the structural circuit that §2.5 BC3 specifies. Respects [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) hypothesis-identity invariant — the projection always anchors at a formation hash. Canonical vocabulary used as written: projection, hypothesis, lifecycle event, formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution, merge, split, substrate, BehavioralCluster.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection.go) — new package + file. `State`, `LifecycleEntry`, `HypothesisProjection`, `ProjectHypothesis`, `ErrFormationNotFound`, `ErrTargetNotFormation`. ~250 lines including doc comments + the two-pass walk + the precedence rules.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/projection_test.go) — **11 tests** covering each terminal state + precedence edges + history ordering + error paths. Imports `internal/hypothesis` (test-time only — the package is in a separate _test file and reuses the writer-side API to populate substrates for projection-side assertions; this is acceptable per the read-only-at-runtime layering choice).
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/hypothesis-state/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/hypothesis-state/main.go) — new binary; tenth operational CLI; first read-only-classification binary.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — new `hypothesis-state-build` target; help text updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`hypothesis-state` CLI section with state-precedence-rules explainer + scope-per-§0051 disclaimer.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0051 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **216 tests** across the service (up from 205 at [`§0050`](#0050--sixth-final-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclustersplit-lands-structural-inverse-of-merge-6-of-6-lifecycle-operations-now-structurally-observable-25-lifecycle-surface-complete); +11 = 11 projection tests). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **Tenth operational binary lands; first read-only binary.** `cmd/` now contains ten binaries. The classification surface widens: substrate-write (six lifecycle operators + Cat II derivation = seven), substrate-audit (`verify` + `orphan-cleanup` = two), projection-read (`hypothesis-state` = one — this entry). Future projection-layer surfaces (multi-hypothesis aggregate query, projection-side caching, projection-driven inference orchestration) extend the read-only classification.
  - **§2.5 BC3 deferred-state read shape now operationally observable.** The Charter prose at §2.5 BC3 has been structurally observable since §0046 (the first lifecycle event reaching a formation), but until this entry no producer-of-the-projection existed. Operators could not ask "is hypothesis X currently promoted?" — they could only ask "what lifecycle events touch X?" and reconstruct the answer by hand. This entry closes that gap.
  - **Layering pattern established for future read-side packages.** `internal/projection` is the first package in the read-only classification; the layering choice (depend on substrate + genproto, NOT on hypothesis or other writer-side packages) is the template subsequent read-side landings will follow. The duplication of message-type discriminator constants is structurally minimal and acceptable per CLAUDE.md §7 — drift surfaces at compile time the moment either side updates a constant.
  - **Precedence rules surface as an operational convention.** The chosen `State` precedence (dissolution > split > merge > demoted > promoted > forming) is an operational convention of THIS projection, not a Charter invariant or Ontology commitment. Future projection surfaces (aggregate queries, alternative read shapes) MAY surface different precedence rules; the §2.5 BC3 + ontology contract only requires that the projection be reconstructible from the substrate — not that a unique precedence be chosen.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/hypothesis-state` without `-formation-event-hash` exits 2; (b) `bin/hypothesis-state -formation-event-hash 00...00 -db /tmp/empty.db` exits 3 with `ErrFormationNotFound`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Multi-hypothesis aggregate queries** — "list every currently-promoted hypothesis", "every hypothesis dissolved in the last 7 days", "every merge produced by inference process X". These are projection-over-many-formations queries; the current `ProjectHypothesis` is one-formation-at-a-time. A follow-on landing introduces aggregate query surfaces if/when operational pressure surfaces them.
    - **Projection-side indexing / caching** — the on-demand linear walk is correct but cost-linear in substrate size. A materialized projection table (or per-formation index) would amortize repeated reads; deferred per the constitutional-minimalism choice above.
    - **Projection over Cat II constructs** — Category II `OperationalSession` records produced by `cmd/derive-operational-session` per [`§0043`](#0043--first-category-ii-operational-construct-lands-padded-v1-canonical-operational-definition-internalderivation--cmdderive-operational-session-introduced) have no lifecycle events (Cat II is one-shot derivation), so a projection over Cat II is structurally trivial; deferred until operator demand surfaces.
    - **Projection-driven inference orchestration** — using projection state to decide when to fire formation patterns, when to promote, when to dissolve. This is the inference-orchestration layer per [`entity-model.md` Category III](../ontology/entity-model.md); requires its own design entry and is out of scope here.
    - **Other concrete Cat III subtypes' projection** — `AutomationGroup`, `CoordinationRing`, `CampaignHypothesis` per [`entity-model.md` Category III](../ontology/entity-model.md) will each need their own lifecycle event types + projection branch. The pattern established here (per-subtype constants + per-lifecycle-event walk) is the template.
    - **Layer B deep-criterion (§2.6)** — unchanged from [`§0047`](#0047--third-category-iii-lifecycle-event-behavioralclusterdemotion-lands-promotedemote-loop-closed-0011-layer-a-cadence-gate-operationally-surfaced) carry-forward; the projection does NOT currently report evidential-independence-driven Layer B candidacy because §2.6 has not yet redacted.

- **Supersession:** None. This entry opens the read-side surface that §0045–§0050 each named as a deferred carry-forward; no prior decision reversed. Establishes a new package (`internal/projection`) + a new classification (read-only binaries) that future entries extend rather than supersede. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.

## `0052` — Multi-hypothesis aggregate projection (`ListHypotheses` + `ProjectAll`); §0051 named carry-forward discharged; substrate-linear-not-quadratic walk

- **Status:** accepted.
- **Date:** 2026-05-20.

- **Context:** [`§0051`](#0051--first-projection-layer-landing-hypothesisprojection-single-hypothesis-read-side-materialization-over-the-25-lifecycle-event-chain-charter-25-bc3-deferred-state-read-shape-now-operationally-observable) landed single-hypothesis projection and named **"Multi-hypothesis aggregate queries"** as the first deferred carry-forward — operator questions like "list every currently-promoted hypothesis" or "every dissolved hypothesis in the last 7 days" require ranging over the projection of every formation. The naive composition (loop over every formation, call `ProjectHypothesis` per formation) is quadratic in substrate size: each `ProjectHypothesis` walks the full substrate, so N formations × M substrate rows = O(N·M). This entry lands a substrate-linear (O(M)) aggregate projection surface that runs a single two-pass walk regardless of formation count.

  Two structural choices decided in this entry:

  - **ProjectAll layering vs ListHypotheses convenience.** The substrate-linear walk is the structurally-meaningful primitive (`ProjectAll(ctx, sub) → map[formationHash]HypothesisProjection`); `ListHypotheses` is a thin paging/filtering wrapper on top. Both are exported because operators benefit from both (`ProjectAll` for in-process callers that already want the map keyed by hash; `ListHypotheses` for callers that want a deterministically-ordered slice with filtering/paging). The duplication is one indirection, not a constitutional one.

  - **Paging shape: Limit/Offset vs cursor-based.** Option (A): `Limit` (cap) + `Offset` (skip first N) over the deterministically-sorted projection slice. Option (B): cursor-based paging that resumes correctly across substrate growth between calls. Option (A) chosen for inception-phase posture per [`§0027`](#0027--substrate-technology-protocol-buffer-canonical-substrate-on-sqlite-blob-store-third-technology-rfc-per-0022-pivot); cursor-based paging is materially more complex (requires either an opaque cursor token whose semantics survive walk reordering, or a stable per-formation "page key" that survives the substrate-position-independent ordering chosen at §0051). Deferred until operational pressure surfaces.

- **Decision:** Land `ProjectAll` + `ListHypotheses` + `ListOptions` in `internal/projection`, with the following structural moves:

  1. **`ProjectAll(ctx, sub) (map[[32]byte]HypothesisProjection, error)`** at [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/list.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/list.go). Two-pass walk over the substrate via `substrate.WalkEvents`:
     - **Pass one:** dispatch formation + promotion rows. Initialize per-formation projection skeletons (keyed by formation event hash); build a `promotionHash → formationHash` index needed in pass two.
     - **Pass two:** dispatch demotion (resolved via the promotion-index to its formation), dissolution (targets formation directly), merge (reaches each antecedent formation), split (targets antecedent formation).
     - Sort each per-formation `LifecycleHistory` ascending by `event_time`; compute terminal `State` via the same precedence rules established at §0051 (`computeState`).

     The two-pass walk has the same structural shape as `ProjectHypothesis`'s two passes but applies them once at the substrate level rather than once per formation. Linear in substrate size (each row visited twice), independent of formation count.

  2. **`ListHypotheses(ctx, sub, opts ListOptions) ([]HypothesisProjection, error)`** at the same file. Calls `ProjectAll`, then:
     - **State filter:** drop projections whose `State` does not equal `opts.StateFilter` (empty string disables).
     - **Deterministic ordering:** ascending lex order of formation event hash. Substrate-position-independent — repeating the call against the same substrate returns projections in the same order regardless of commit order. This is the canonical "list view" ordering for inception-phase tooling.
     - **Paging:** apply `Offset` (skip first N) then `Limit` (cap returned length). Zero values disable each.

  3. **`cmd/list-hypotheses` operator interface** at [`services/ingestion/cmd/list-hypotheses/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/list-hypotheses/main.go). Eleventh operational binary; **second in the read-only classification** (alongside `hypothesis-state` from §0051). Options: `-state` (one of `forming`, `promoted`, `demoted`, `dissolved`, `merged_into`, `split_into`; empty disables); `-limit` (default 0 = unbounded); `-offset` (default 0). Structured JSON array output, one element per projection sharing the shape of `hypothesis-state`'s single-projection output. Exit codes: **0** success (including empty); **2** tool/configuration error (e.g. invalid `-state` value).

- **Constitutional review:** No Charter invariant amended. No frozen-section prose modified. Respects [§2.1](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#21-observational-integrity) — read-only over immutable substrate; no lifecycle event written, modified, or interpreted in a §2.1-violating way. Respects [§2.2](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#22-epistemic-separation) — aggregate `State` field is an operational summary across the lifecycle chain, NOT a category claim about the underlying phenomenon. Respects [§2.3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#23-provenance-integrity) — every projection entry references its producing lifecycle event by content-hash; the aggregate projection is reconstructible from the same substrate state regardless of when or where it is computed. Respects [§2.5](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — projects exactly the six lifecycle operations defined at the abstract `Hypothesis` type level. Respects [§2.5 BC3](../charter/constitutional-charter.md#25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness) — extends the BC3-mandated read shape from one-formation-at-a-time to all-formations-at-once without storing aggregate state. Respects [`§0045`](#0045--first-category-iii-hypothesis-subtype-behavioralcluster-formation-lands-25-hypothesis-lifecycle-explicitness-structurally-observable) — every projection anchors at a formation hash. Respects [`§0051`](#0051--first-projection-layer-landing-hypothesisprojection-single-hypothesis-read-side-materialization-over-the-25-lifecycle-event-chain-charter-25-bc3-deferred-state-read-shape-now-operationally-observable) — same `State` enum, same `LifecycleEntry` shape, same precedence rules; the aggregate surface is a structural extension, not a divergence. Equivalence verified at test time (`TestListEquivalentToPerFormationProject`): per-formation projection from `ProjectAll` is byte-equal to `ProjectHypothesis(sameFormation)`. Canonical vocabulary used as written: projection, hypothesis, lifecycle event, formation, promotion, demotion, dissolution, merge, split, substrate, BehavioralCluster.

- **Consequences:**
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/list.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/list.go) — new file. `ProjectAll`, `ListHypotheses`, `ListOptions`. ~250 lines including doc comments + the two-pass walk + the filtering/paging logic.
  - [`services/ingestion/internal/projection/list_test.go`](../../services/ingestion/internal/projection/list_test.go) — **8 tests** covering empty substrate, mixed-state populations, state filter, ordering stability (substrate-position-independent), limit + offset paging, offset-beyond-length, ProjectAll-equivalent-to-per-formation-ProjectHypothesis (the structural-equivalence invariant), all-six-end-states aggregate stress test.
  - [`services/ingestion/cmd/list-hypotheses/main.go`](../../services/ingestion/cmd/list-hypotheses/main.go) — new binary; eleventh operational CLI; second read-only binary.
  - [`services/ingestion/Makefile`](../../services/ingestion/Makefile) — new `list-hypotheses-build` target; help text updated.
  - [`services/ingestion/README.md`](../../services/ingestion/README.md) — new §`list-hypotheses` CLI section with paging-vs-cursor scope disclaimer + linear-walk-explainer.
  - [`docs/charter/decision-log.md`](./decision-log.md) §0052 (this entry).
  - **Test count grows.** Combined: **224 tests** across the service (up from 216 at [`§0051`](#0051--first-projection-layer-landing-hypothesisprojection-single-hypothesis-read-side-materialization-over-the-25-lifecycle-event-chain-charter-25-bc3-deferred-state-read-shape-now-operationally-observable); +8 = 8 list tests). All passing under `go test -race ./...`.
  - **Zero external dependencies added.**
  - **§0051 named carry-forward discharged.** "Multi-hypothesis aggregate queries (e.g. 'list every currently-promoted hypothesis', 'every hypothesis dissolved in the last 7 days', 'every merge produced by inference process X')" — the first two examples are now directly supported via `-state` filter + paging. The third (merge-by-producer) requires additional projection state that does not yet exist (the merge event does not currently carry an "inference process" attribute); deferred along with the broader projection-driven-inference-orchestration carry-forward.
  - **Eleventh operational binary lands; second read-only binary.** `cmd/` now contains: substrate-write (7), substrate-audit/maintenance (2), projection-read (2 — `hypothesis-state` and `list-hypotheses`).
  - **Substrate-linear walk pattern established.** `ProjectAll`'s two-pass shape is the template for future aggregate read-side surfaces (e.g. cross-subtype aggregate projection once additional Cat III subtypes land). The structural lesson: any aggregate over substrate-immutable events SHOULD walk the substrate once with per-target-projection dispatch, never N times with per-target filtering — the former is linear in M (substrate size); the latter is quadratic in N·M.
  - **Equivalence invariant tested.** `TestListEquivalentToPerFormationProject` asserts that the per-formation projection from `ProjectAll` is byte-equal to `ProjectHypothesis(sameFormation)` for the same substrate state. This invariant defends against precedence-rule drift or LifecycleHistory-shape drift between the single-formation and aggregate code paths. Future projection-side optimizations (caching, incremental updates, indexing) MUST preserve this invariant.
  - **Smoke test of binary.** Two invocations validated at commit time: (a) `bin/list-hypotheses -state bogus` exits 2 with explanatory message; (b) `bin/list-hypotheses -db /tmp/empty.db` exits 0 with `[]`.
  - **Out of scope at this layer (carry-forwards).**
    - **Cursor-based paging** — Limit/Offset works for static substrate state but does not resume cleanly across substrate growth between calls (rows added between calls shift the offset semantics). Cursor paging deferred until operator pressure surfaces.
    - **Time-window filtering** — e.g. "every formation created in the last 24 hours", "every hypothesis dissolved in the last 7 days". The `LifecycleHistory` carries the timestamps; a time-window filter is straightforward to add when needed.
    - **Sort orders beyond formation-hash ascending** — e.g. by latest-event-time, by state, by promotion cadence. Multi-key sorting is straightforward to add when needed; one canonical order suffices at this layer.
    - **Aggregate counters / histograms** — "how many hypotheses are in each state", "histogram of formation-to-promotion latency". These are derived statistics over the aggregate projection; can be computed by callers from the returned list, or added as a separate read-side surface.
    - **Projection-side caching / incremental updates** — unchanged from [`§0051`](#0051--first-projection-layer-landing-hypothesisprojection-single-hypothesis-read-side-materialization-over-the-25-lifecycle-event-chain-charter-25-bc3-deferred-state-read-shape-now-operationally-observable) carry-forward.
    - **Multi-subtype aggregate** — once additional Cat III concrete subtypes land (`AutomationGroup`, `CoordinationRing`, `CampaignHypothesis`), `ProjectAll` will need a sibling per subtype, OR a generic abstract surface above the per-subtype projections. Deferred until the second concrete subtype lands.
    - **Cross-operation provenance queries** — "which merge produced this formation", "which split's antecedent contained this formation". The substrate carries the structural references; projection-side surfaces for these queries are deferred.

- **Supersession:** None. Extends the read-side surface opened at [`§0051`](#0051--first-projection-layer-landing-hypothesisprojection-single-hypothesis-read-side-materialization-over-the-25-lifecycle-event-chain-charter-25-bc3-deferred-state-read-shape-now-operationally-observable); no prior decision reversed. The equivalence invariant ensures the single-formation path (§0051) and the aggregate path (this entry) remain in structural agreement. [`§0022`](#0022--implementation-pivot-22-implementation-against-current-structural-ground-authorized-storage-technology-deferral-reversed-by-authorization) implementation-gate criteria continue to be satisfied.
